USER @transaction.atomic
USER def review_resumption(
USER     actor_id: int,
USER     application_id: int,
USER     actual_resumption_date: date,
USER ) -> None:
USER     """
USER     Review the submitted resumption and adjust balance for early or late return.
USER     
USER     Preconditions:
USER     - A valid resumption submission exists (status 'submitted' or similar).
USER     - Actor is the applicable Sanctioning Authority.
USER     
USER     Postconditions:
USER     - If actual date matches approved end date: No adjustment.
USER     - If actual date is earlier/late: Adjust balance and record actual leave period.
USER     - History entry appended.
USER     
USER     Raises:
USER         ValueError: If application status not allowed or date invalid.
USER     """
USER     resumption_app = LeaveApplication.objects.get(id=application_id)
USER 
USER     category_enum = ModelLeaveCategory(resumption_app.category)
USER     
USER     # Check if category allows resumption review (EL, COL, VL)
USER     if category_enum not in [ModelLeaveCategory.EL, ModelLeaveCategory.COL, ModelLeaveCategory.VL]:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Resumption review is only applicable for EL, COL, or VL categories.")
USER 
USER     allowed_statuses = ['submitted']  # Assuming 'submitted' represents the resumption review state
USER     if resumption_app.status not in allowed_statuses:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Cannot review resumption for application in status {resumption_app.status}.")
USER 
USER     # Compare actual date with approved end date (requested_to)
USER     approved_end_date = resumption_app.requested_to
USER     
USER     if actual_resumption_date == approved_end_date:
USER         description = f"Resumption date matches approved end date ({approved_end_date}). No adjustment required."
USER     elif actual_resumption_date < approved_end_date:
USER         # Early return: Leave ended before approved. 
USER         # Balance adjustment: The employee used fewer days than approved? Or the approved period was extended?
USER         # Usually, if you return early, the leave period is shortened. 
USER         # We need to adjust the balance for the unused portion? Or the consumed portion?
USER         # BR-EL-027/028: "adjusts balance accordingly".
USER         # If returned early, the employee has not used the full approved period. 
USER         # So we should add back the unused days to available balance?
USER         # But wait, the leave was already taken (used_days increased). 
USER         # If they return early, maybe the 'used_days' is correct for the actual period, and we need to adjust the 'available_days' for the remaining approved period?
USER         # Let's assume: 
USER         # Approved period: [start, end]
USER         # Actual period: [start, actual_end]
USER         # If actual_end < end, then the employee used (actual_end - start) days.
USER         # The approved leave was (end - start + 1) days.
USER         # So unused days = (end - start + 1) - (actual_end - start + 1) = end - actual_end.
USER         # We should add these unused days back to available balance? 
USER         # But the employee already used them. 
USER         # Actually, if they return early, it means they didn't take the full leave. 
USER         # So the 'used_days' in LeaveBalance should reflect the actual usage. 
USER         # If the system recorded used_days based on approved period, we need to correct it.
USER         # Let's assume we adjust the balance by adding back the unused days (end - actual_end).
USER         consumed_days = calculate_consumed_days(approved_end_date, resumption_app.requested_from)
USER         actual_consumed_days = calculate_consumed_days(actual_resumption_date, resumption_app.requested_from)
USER         unused_days = consumed_days - actual_consumed_days
USER         
USER         if unused_days > 0:
USER             _update_balance_on_resumption(resumption_app, actor_id, 'add', unused_days)
USER             description = f"Early return on {actual_resumption_date}. Added {unused_days} days to balance."
USER         else:
USER             description = f"Early return on {actual_resumption_date}. No adjustment (negative unused)."
USER     else:  # actual_resumption_date > approved_end_date
USER         # Late return: Employee took more time than approved.
USER         # This might be an extension or just late return.
USER         # If it's just late return, the leave period was extended de facto.
USER         # We need to adjust balance for the extra days used?
USER         # Or maybe the original approval was wrong?
USER         # Let's assume we add the extra days to used_days (if not already done) and adjust available balance accordingly?
USER         # Actually, if they return late, it means they used more days than approved.
USER         # So we should increase used_days and decrease available_days? 
USER         # But the leave was already taken. 
USER         # Let's assume we calculate the difference and adjust.
USER         consumed_days = calculate_consumed_days(approved_end_date, resumption_app.requested_from)
USER         actual_consumed_days = calculate_consumed_days(actual_resumption_date, resumption_app.requested_from)
USER         extra_days = actual_consumed_days - consumed_days
USER         
USER         if extra_days > 0:
USER             _update_balance_on_resumption(resumption_app, actor_id, 'subtract', extra_days)
USER             description = f"Late return on {actual_resumption_date}. Subtracted {extra_days} days from balance."
USER         else:
USER             description = f"Late return on {actual_resumption_date}. No adjustment (negative extra)."
USER 
USER     # Update the resumption application status to indicate review completion
USER     resumption_app.status = 'approved'  # Or some completed state
USER     resumption_app.save()
USER 
USER     _log_action_history(
USER         application=resumption_app,
USER         actor_id=actor_id,
USER         action_type="resumption_reviewed",
USER         description=description,
USER     )
USER

USER @transaction.atomic
USER def get_balances(user_id: int, year: int) -> List[Tuple[ModelLeaveCategory, float]]:
USER     """
USER     Retrieve leave balances for a user in a specific year.
USER     
USER     Args:
USER         user_id: ID of the employee.
USER         year: Year to query.
USER     
USER     Returns:
USER         List of tuples (category, available_days).
USER     """
USER     from modules.leave.models import LeaveBalance
USER     
USER     balances = LeaveBalance.objects.filter(
USER         user_id=user_id,
USER         year=year
USER     ).values('category', 'available_days')
USER 
USER     result = []
USER     for balance in balances:
USER         category_enum = ModelLeaveCategory(balance['category'])
USER         available = float(balance['available_days'])
USER         result.append((category_enum, available))
USER     
USER     return result
USER

USER @transaction.atomic
USER def get_history(user_id: int) -> List[dict]:
USER     """
USER     Retrieve leave action history for a user.
USER     
USER     Args:
USER         user_id: ID of the employee.
USER     
USER     Returns:
USER         List of dictionaries containing history entries.
USER     """
USER     from modules.leave.models import LeaveActionHistory, LeaveApplication
USER     
USER     # Get all applications for the user
USER     applications = LeaveApplication.objects.filter(user_id=user_id)
USER     
USER     # Get all history entries linked to these applications
USER     history_entries = LeaveActionHistory.objects.filter(
USER         application__in=applications
USER     ).order_by('-created_at')
USER 
USER     result = []
USER     for entry in history_entries:
USER         result.append({
USER             'id': entry.id,
USER             'application_id': entry.application.id,
USER             'actor_id': entry.actor_id,
USER             'action_type': entry.action_type,
USER             'description': entry.description,
USER             'created_at': entry.created_at.isoformat(),
USER         })
USER     
USER     return result
USER

USER def _restore_balance_on_cancellation(cancellation_app: LeaveApplication, actor_id: int) -> None:
USER     """
USER     Restore leave balance when cancellation is approved (BR-EL-023).
USER     
USER     This assumes the original leave was approved and used_days were increased.
USER     On cancellation approval, we need to revert the used_days or adjust available_days.
USER     Since we don't have a direct link between cancellation_app and original_app's balance changes,
USER     we'll assume the cancellation_app represents the reversal.
USER     
USER     We'll update the balance for the category/year of the original leave (which should be same as cancellation_app).
USER     """
USER     from modules.leave.models import LeaveBalance
USER     
USER     # Determine year (simplified: use requested_from year)
USER     year = cancellation_app.requested_from.year
USER     
USER     # Calculate consumed days for the original period (before cancellation)
USER     # We don't have the original application here, but we can assume the cancellation_app 
USER     # has the same category and dates as the original.
USER     # Let's assume we need to restore the balance that was deducted.
USER     # If the original leave was approved, used_days were increased.
USER     # On cancellation approval, we should decrease used_days or increase available_days.
USER     # Let's increase available_days by the number of days in the original period (assuming full usage was recorded).
USER     # Or better: calculate the difference between approved and actual usage? 
USER     # But for cancellation, it's a reversal of the entire approved leave.
USER     
USER     # We'll assume the balance record exists or create it.
USER     # Let's add back the days that were used (assuming full usage was recorded).
USER     # Or simply set available_days to the original entitlement?
USER     # To be safe, let's calculate consumed days for the period and add them back.
USER     
USER     from datetime import timedelta
USER     
USER     start_date = cancellation_app.requested_from
USER     end_date = cancellation_app.requested_to
USER     
USER     total_days = (end_date - start_date).days + 1
USER     
USER     # Query or create balance record
USER     try:
USER         balance = LeaveBalance.objects.get(
USER             user_id=actor_id,
USER             year=year,
USER             category=cancellation_app.category
USER         )
USER     except LeaveBalance.DoesNotExist:
USER         balance = LeaveBalance.objects.create(
USER             user_id=actor_id,
USER             year=year,
USER             category=cancellation_app.category,
USER             available_days=0.0,
USER             used_days=0.0
USER         )
USER     
USER     # Restore the balance (add back the used days)
USER     # Note: This is a simplification. In reality, we might need to know how many days were actually used before cancellation.
USER     # Assuming full usage was recorded for an approved leave.
USER     balance.available_days += float(total_days)
USER     balance.save()
USER

USER     Placeholder for extension balance check.

USER def _update_balance_on_resumption(resumption_app: LeaveApplication, actor_id: int, adjustment_type: str, days: float) -> None:
USER     """
USER     Update leave balance on resumption review (BR-EL-027/028).
USER     
USER     Args:
USER         resumption_app: The resumption application.
USER         actor_id: ID of the Sanctioning Authority.
USER         adjustment_type: 'add' or 'subtract'.
USER         days: Number of days to adjust.
USER     """
USER     from modules.leave.models import LeaveBalance
USER     
USER     year = resumption_app.requested_from.year
USER     category_enum = ModelLeaveCategory(resumption_app.category)
USER     
USER     try:
USER         balance = LeaveBalance.objects.get(
USER             user_id=actor_id,
USER             year=year,
USER             category=category_enum
USER         )
USER     except LeaveBalance.DoesNotExist:
USER         balance = LeaveBalance.objects.create(
USER             user_id=actor_id,
USER             year=year,
USER             category=category_enum,
USER             available_days=0.0,
USER             used_days=0.0
USER         )
USER     
USER     if adjustment_type == 'add':
USER         balance.available_days += days
USER     else:  # subtract
USER         balance.available_days -= days
USER     
USER     balance.save()
USER

USER modules/leave/services/applications.py
USER ```
USER """
USER Service layer for leave applications.
USER Implements core business logic: validation, routing, consent handling, and decision recording.
USER Uses Django transactions to ensure atomicity of state changes and history logging.
USER """
USER 
USER from datetime import date
USER from typing import Any, Optional, Tuple
USER 
USER from django.db import transaction, models
USER from django.core.exceptions import ValidationError as DjangoValidationError
USER 
USER from modules.directory.models import UserRef
USER from modules.leave.domain.policy import (
USER     LeaveCategory,
USER     calculate_consumed_days,
USER     check_applicant_overlap,
USER     requires_substitute,
USER     unit_head_decision_required,
USER     get_categories_requiring_unit_head_decision,
USER )
USER from modules.leave.models import (
USER     LeaveApplication,
USER     LeaveBalance,
USER     LeaveActionHistory,
USER     LeaveCategory as ModelLeaveCategory,
USER )
USER from fusion_auth.permissions import HasPermission

USER 
USER def apply_leave(
USER     actor_id: int,
USER     category: str,
USER     requested_from: date,
USER     requested_to: date,
USER     purpose: str,
USER     station_leave_details: Optional[dict] = None,
USER     nominated_substitute_id: Optional[int] = None,
USER ) -> LeaveApplication:
USER     """
USER     Create and submit a new leave application.
USER     
USER     Validates eligibility, balance, overlaps, and substitutes.
USER     Routes to substitute consent if required, otherwise proceeds to Unit Head review.
USER     
USER     Args:
USER         actor_id: ID of the employee submitting the request.
USER         category: Leave category (e.g., 'CL', 'RH').
USER         requested_from: Start date of leave.
USER         requested_to: End date of leave.
USER         purpose: Reason for leave.
USER         station_leave_details: Optional JSON details for station leave.
USER         nominated_substitute_id: ID of the nominated substitute (if applicable).
USER     
USER     Returns:
USER         The created LeaveApplication instance.
USER     
USER     Raises:
USER         ValueError: If validation fails or action is not allowed in current status.
USER         DjangoValidationError: If domain policy rules are violated.
USER     """
USER     # Map string category to Enum
USER     try:
USER         category_enum = ModelLeaveCategory(category)
USER     except ValueError:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Invalid leave category: {category}")
USER 
USER     application, created = LeaveApplication.objects.get_or_create(
USER         user_id=actor_id,
USER         defaults={
USER             'category': category_enum,
USER             'requested_from': requested_from,
USER             'requested_to': requested_to,
USER             'purpose': purpose,
USER             'station_leave_details': station_leave_details,
USER             'nominated_substitute_id': nominated_substitute_id,
USER             'status': 'draft',
USER         },
USER     )
USER 
USER     if not created:
USER         # Check if status allows modification (only 'draft' or 'submitted' before full processing)
USER         # However, per BR-EL-034, in-place modification is blocked. 
USER         # We assume this function is called only for new applications.
USER         # If an existing draft exists, we might need to handle it differently, 
USER         # but the spec implies a fresh application flow.
USER         pass
USER 
USER     # Domain Validation
USER     try:
USER         policy = get_policy_config(category_enum)
USER         
USER         # Check balance (simplified: assume sufficient balance if created or updated by service)
USER         # In a full system, we would query LeaveBalance for the user/year/category.
USER         # For this service layer, we assume the balance check is handled or passed.
USER         # We will perform a placeholder check to ensure logic exists.
USER         _check_balance(actor_id, category_enum, requested_from, requested_to)
USER 
USER         # Check overlap (simplified: assume no active leave for actor)
USER         _check_overlap(actor_id, requested_from, requested_to)
USER 
USER         # Check substitute eligibility if required
USER         if requires_substitute(category_enum):
USER             if nominated_substitute_id is None:
USER                 raise ValueError("Substitute must be nominated for this category.")
USER             _validate_substitute_eligibility(nominated_substitute_id, actor_id, requested_from, requested_to)
USER     except (ValueError, DjangoValidationError) as e:
USER         # Log history entry for validation failure before raising
USER         _log_action_history(
USER             application=application,
USER             actor_id=actor_id,
USER             action_type="validation_failed",
USER             description=str(e),
USER         )
USER         raise
USER 
USER     # Create the application instance (if not already created above)
USER     if not created:
USER         application.save()
USER 
USER     # Determine routing path
USER     if requires_substitute(category_enum):
USER         # Route to substitute consent
USER         status = 'submitted'
USER         _log_action_history(
USER             application=application,
USER             actor_id=actor_id,
USER             action_type="substitute_consent_required",
USER             description=f"Application submitted. Awaiting substitute consent.",
USER         )
USER     else:
USER         # Route to Unit Head review (or Director self-sanction if applicable)
USER         status = 'submitted'
USER         _log_action_history(
USER             application=application,
USER             actor_id=actor_id,
USER             action_type="submitted_for_review",
USER             description=f"Application submitted for {category_enum.value} leave.",
USER         )
USER 
USER     # Update status to submitted
USER     application.status = status
USER     application.save()
USER 
USER     return application
USER 
USER 
USER def respond_to_substitute_request(
USER     actor_id: int,
USER     application_id: int,
USER     accept: bool,
USER ) -> None:
USER     """
USER     Accept or decline a substitute request.
USER     
USER     Args:
USER         actor_id: ID of the substitute (or responsibility holder).
USER         application_id: ID of the associated leave application.
USER         accept: True to accept, False to decline.
USER     
USER     Raises:
USER         ValueError: If action is not allowed in current status.
USER     """
USER     application = LeaveApplication.objects.get(id=application_id)
USER 
USER     if application.status not in ['submitted', 'under_review']:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Cannot respond to substitute request for application in status {application.status}.")
USER 
USER     # Update application status based on response
USER     if accept:
USER         new_status = 'under_review'
USER         description = "Substitute consent accepted."
USER     else:
USER         new_status = 'submitted'  # Reverts to applicant action required state effectively
USER         description = "Substitute consent declined."
USER 
USER     application.status = new_status
USER     application.save()
USER 
USER     _log_action_history(
USER         application=application,
USER         actor_id=actor_id,
USER         action_type="substitute_response",
USER         description=description,
USER     )
USER 
USER 
USER def nominate_replacement_substitute(
USER     actor_id: int,
USER     application_id: int,
USER     new_substitute_id: int,
USER ) -> None:
USER     """
USER     Nominate a replacement substitute after a decline.
USER     
USER     Args:
USER         actor_id: ID of the employee (applicant).
USER         application_id: ID of the associated leave application.
USER         new_substitute_id: ID of the new nominated substitute.
USER     
USER     Raises:
USER         ValueError: If action is not allowed in current status.
USER     """
USER     application = LeaveApplication.objects.get(id=application_id)
USER 
USER     # Check if we are in a state where replacement is needed (e.g., after decline)
USER     # Per EL-UC-019, this happens when substitute declines and request enters 'Applicant Action Required'.
USER     # We assume status reflects this or is 'submitted' awaiting another response.
USER     if application.status not in ['submitted', 'under_review']:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Cannot nominate replacement substitute for application in status {application.status}.")
USER 
USER     # Validate new substitute eligibility (simplified)
USER     _validate_substitute_eligibility(new_substitute_id, actor_id, application.requested_from, application.requested_to)
USER 
USER     # Update the nominated substitute ID
USER     application.nominated_substitute_id = new_substitute_id
USER     application.save()
USER 
USER     _log_action_history(
USER         application=application,
USER         actor_id=actor_id,
USER         action_type="substitute_renomination",
USER         description=f"New substitute {new_substitute_id} nominated.",
USER     )
USER 
USER 
USER @transaction.atomic
USER def unit_head_review(
USER     actor_id: int,
USER     application_id: int,
USER     decision: str,
USER ) -> None:
USER     """
USER     Record Unit Head's review decision.
USER     
USER     For CL/RH: Approve or Reject.
USER     For SCL/EL/COL/VL: Recommended or Not Recommended.
USER     
USER     Args:
USER         actor_id: ID of the Unit Head.
USER         application_id: ID of the leave application.
USER         decision: 'approve', 'reject', 'recommended', or 'not_recommended'.
USER     
USER     Raises:
USER         ValueError: If action is not allowed in current status.
USER     """
USER     application = LeaveApplication.objects.get(id=application_id)
USER 
USER     # Determine expected statuses based on category and decision type
USER     category_enum = ModelLeaveCategory(application.category)
USER     
USER     if unit_head_decision_required(category_enum):
USER         # CL/RH: Unit Head makes final decision
USER         allowed_statuses = ['submitted', 'under_review']
USER         valid_decisions = ['approve', 'reject']
USER     else:
USER         # SCL/EL/COL/VL: Unit Head recommends
USER         allowed_statuses = ['submitted', 'under_review']
USER         valid_decisions = ['recommended', 'not_recommended']
USER 
USER     if application.status not in allowed_statuses:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Unit Head can only review applications in status {allowed_statuses}. Current status: {application.status}")
USER 
USER     if decision not in valid_decisions:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Invalid decision '{decision}' for category {category_enum.value}.")
USER 
USER     # Update application status and record history
USER     if decision == 'approve':
USER         new_status = 'approved'
USER         description = f"Unit Head approved {application.category.value} leave."
USER     elif decision == 'reject':
USER         new_status = 'rejected'
USER         description = f"Unit Head rejected {application.category.value} leave."
USER     elif decision == 'recommended':
USER         new_status = 'recommended'
USER         description = f"Unit Head recommended {application.category.value} leave for higher sanction."
USER     else:  # not_recommended
USER         new_status = 'not_recommended'
USER         description = f"Unit Head not recommended {application.category.value} leave for higher sanction."
USER 
USER     application.status = new_status
USER     application.save()
USER 
USER     _log_action_history(
USER         application=application,
USER         actor_id=actor_id,
USER         action_type="unit_head_decision",
USER         description=description,
USER     )
USER 
USER 
USER @transaction.atomic
USER def establishment_verify_and_route(
USER     actor_id: int,
USER     application_id: int,
USER ) -> None:
USER     """
USER     Perform Establishment-level verification and route to final Sanctioning Authority.
USER     
USER     This is a placeholder for the logic defined in BR-EL-018 and EL-UC-004.
USER     In this implementation, it assumes a simple routing step where the application
USER     is verified (e.g., completeness check) and then routed based on configuration.
USER     
USER     Args:
USER         actor_id: ID of the Establishment Processing Role (e.g., Deputy Registrar).
USER         application_id: ID of the leave application.
USER     
USER     Raises:
USER         ValueError: If action is not allowed in current status.
USER     """
USER     application = LeaveApplication.objects.get(id=application_id)
USER 
USER     # Check if this is a higher-sanction case requiring routing
USER     category_enum = ModelLeaveCategory(application.category)
USER     if unit_head_decision_required(category_enum):
USER         raise ValueError(f"Unit Head decision required for {category_enum.value}, no establishment routing needed.")
USER 
USER     allowed_statuses = ['recommended', 'not_recommended']
USER     if application.status not in allowed_statuses:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Establishment can only process applications in status {allowed_statuses}. Current status: {application.status}")
USER 
USER     # Placeholder verification logic (e.g., check for required fields)
USER     # In a real system, this would involve complex hierarchy configuration checks.
USER     _verify_establishment_requirements(application)
USER 
USER     # Route to final Sanctioning Authority (simulated by updating status)
USER     # The actual routing might involve creating a work item or changing status directly.
USER     # Per BR-EL-019, we resolve the competent authority. Here, we assume the next step is final sanction.
USER     new_status = 'under_review'  # Or a specific 'awaiting_final_sanction' if defined
USER     description = f"Establishment verified and routed to final Sanctioning Authority."
USER 
USER     application.status = new_status
USER     application.save()
USER 
USER     _log_action_history(
USER         application=application,
USER         actor_id=actor_id,
USER         action_type="establishment_routing",
USER         description=description,
USER     )
USER 
USER 
USER @transaction.atomic
USER def final_sanction_decision(
USER     actor_id: int,
USER     application_id: int,
USER     decision: str,
USER ) -> None:
USER     """
USER     Record the final sanctioning decision.
USER     
USER     Approve or Reject. Updates balance on approval.
USER     
USER     Args:
USER         actor_id: ID of the Sanctioning Authority (e.g., Dean, Registrar, Director).
USER         application_id: ID of the leave application.
USER         decision: 'approve' or 'reject'.
USER     
USER     Raises:
USER         ValueError: If action is not allowed in current status.
USER     """
USER     application = LeaveApplication.objects.get(id=application_id)
USER 
USER     # Check if this is a final sanction case (not Unit Head decision)
USER     category_enum = ModelLeaveCategory(application.category)
USER     if unit_head_decision_required(category_enum):
USER         raise ValueError(f"Unit Head makes the final decision for {category_enum.value}, no higher authority needed.")
USER 
USER     allowed_statuses = ['recommended', 'not_recommended']
USER     if application.status not in allowed_statuses:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Final Sanctioning Authority can only process applications in status {allowed_statuses}. Current status: {application.status}")
USER 
USER     if decision not in ['approve', 'reject']:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Invalid final decision '{decision}'. Must be 'approve' or 'reject'.")
USER 
USER     # Update application status
USER     new_status = 'approved' if decision == 'approve' else 'rejected'
USER     description = f"Final Sanctioning Authority {decision}ed {application.category.value} leave."
USER 
USER     application.status = new_status
USER     application.save()
USER 
USER     # Handle balance update on approval (BR-EL-025, BR-EL-023)
USER     if decision == 'approve':
USER         _update_balance_on_approval(application, actor_id)
USER 
USER     _log_action_history(
USER         application=application,
USER         actor_id=actor_id,
USER         action_type="final_sanction",
USER         description=description,
USER     )
USER

USER     #    OR adjust used_days/available_days based on cancellation/extension rules.
USER     # 5. Save LeaveBalance.
USER     pass

USER def _log_action_history(
USER     application: LeaveApplication,
USER     actor_id: int,
USER     action_type: str,
USER     description: str,
USER ) -> None:
USER     """
USER     Append an entry to the leave action history (BR-EL-037).
USER     
USER     This function is transaction-safe as it's called within atomic blocks.
USER     """
USER     history = LeaveActionHistory.objects.create(
USER         application=application,
USER         actor_id=actor_id,
USER         action_type=action_type,
USER         description=description,
USER     )

USER def withdraw_pending_request(actor_id: int, application_id: int) -> None:
USER     """Withdraw a pending leave application."""
USER     try:
USER         application = LeaveApplication.objects.get(id=application_id)
USER         
USER         # Check if the application belongs to the actor (optional but good practice)
USER         # and if it is in a withdrawable state (e.g., 'pending')
USER         if application.user_id != actor_id:
USER             raise ValueError("You can only withdraw your own applications.")
USER         
USER         if application.status not in ['pending', 'draft']:  # Adjust based on actual status enum
USER             raise ValueError(f"Cannot withdraw application with status {application.status}.")
USER             
USER         # Update status to withdrawn
USER         application.status = 'withdrawn'
USER         application.save()
USER         
USER         # Log the action
USER         history = LeaveActionHistory.objects.create(
USER             application_id=application_id,
USER             actor_id=actor_id,
USER             action_type='withdrawal',
USER             description=f"Application withdrawn by user {actor_id}"
USER         )
USER     except LeaveApplication.DoesNotExist:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Application with ID {application_id} not found.")
USER     except ValidationError as e:
USER         raise ValueError(str(e))
USER 
USER 
USER def request_cancellation(actor_id: int, application_id: int) -> None:
USER     """Request cancellation of an approved leave."""
USER     try:
USER         application = LeaveApplication.objects.get(id=application_id)
USER         
USER         if application.user_id != actor_id:
USER             raise ValueError("You can only cancel your own applications.")
USER             
USER         # Update status to 'cancellation_requested' or similar
USER         application.status = 'cancellation_requested'
USER         application.save()
USER         
USER         history = LeaveActionHistory.objects.create(
USER             application_id=application_id,
USER             actor_id=actor_id,
USER             action_type='cancellation_request',
USER             description=f"Cancellation requested by user {actor_id}"
USER         )
USER     except LeaveApplication.DoesNotExist:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Application with ID {application_id} not found.")
USER 
USER 
USER def decide_cancellation(actor_id: int, application_id: int, decision: str) -> None:
USER     """Decide on a cancellation request."""
USER     try:
USER         application = LeaveApplication.objects.get(id=application_id)
USER         
USER         if application.status != 'cancellation_requested':
USER             raise ValueError(f"Cannot decide cancellation for application with status {application.status}.")
USER             
USER         if decision == 'approve':
USER             # Restore balance and mark as cancelled
USER             _update_balance_on_cancellation(application, actor_id)
USER             application.status = 'cancelled'
USER             description = f"Cancellation approved by {actor_id}."
USER         else:  # reject
USER             application.status = 'approved'  # Revert to original status or keep pending? Usually revert.
USER             description = f"Cancellation rejected by {actor_id}."
USER             
USER         application.save()
USER         
USER         history = LeaveActionHistory.objects.create(
USER             application_id=application_id,
USER             actor_id=actor_id,
USER             action_type='cancellation_decision',
USER             description=description
USER         )
USER     except LeaveApplication.DoesNotExist:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Application with ID {application_id} not found.")
USER

USER def request_extension(actor_id: int, application_id: int, extension_from: date, extension_to: date, purpose: str, station_leave_details: Any = None, nominated_substitute_id: int = None) -> None:

USER         application.requested_to = extension_to
USER         application.purpose = purpose
USER         application.station_leave_details = station_leave_details

USER         
USER         history = LeaveActionHistory.objects.create(
USER             application_id=application_id,
USER             actor_id=actor_id,
USER             action_type='extension_request',
USER             description=f"Extension requested from {extension_from} to {extension_to}"
USER         )
USER     except LeaveApplication.DoesNotExist:

USER def submit_resumption(actor_id: int, application_id: int, actual_resumption_date: date) -> None:
USER     """Submit actual resumption of duty information."""

USER

USER         application.actual_resumption_date = actual_resumption_date
USER         application.save()
USER         
USER         history = LeaveActionHistory.objects.create(
USER             application_id=application_id,
USER             actor_id=actor_id,
USER             action_type='resumption_submitted',
USER             description=f"Resumption submitted on {actual_resumption_date}"

USER         
USER         # Adjust balance based on actual resumption date vs expected
USER         _update_balance_on_resumption(application, actor_id, actual_resumption_date)
USER         
USER         history = LeaveActionHistory.objects.create(
USER             application_id=application_id,
USER             actor_id=actor_id,
USER             action_type='resumption_reviewed',
USER             description=f"Resumption reviewed on {actual_resumption_date}"
USER         )
USER     except LeaveApplication.DoesNotExist:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Application with ID {application_id} not found.")

ASSISTANT def route_to_approver(app: LeaveApplication) -> None:
ASSISTANT     """Route the application to the appropriate approver based on hierarchy.
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     - If unit_head_id == user_id (self-sanctioning authority): go to SANCTION.

ASSISTANT     """

ASSISTANT     if requires_substitute(ModelLeaveCategory(app.category)):
ASSISTANT         # If a substitute is nominated, route to SUBSTITUTE stage
ASSISTANT         app.stage = ModelLeaveCategory.SUBSTITUTE.value  # Assuming SUBSTITUTE is a valid Stage choice
ASSISTANT         app.assignee_id = app.substitute_id
ASSISTANT         app.substitute_status = "pending"
ASSISTANT     else:
ASSISTANT         # Default routing logic for non-substitute categories
ASSISTANT         if app.unit_head_id == app.user_id:
ASSISTANT             # Self-sanctioning authority (e.g., Director)
ASSISTANT             app.stage = ModelLeaveCategory.SANCTION.value
ASSISTANT             app.assignee_id = app.user_id
ASSISTANT         elif app.sanctioning_authority_id is not None:
ASSISTANT             # Standard hierarchy: Unit Head -> Establishment -> Sanctioning Authority
ASSISTANT             # Per DESIGN.md, we route to UNIT_HEAD first for review/recommendation

ASSISTANT             app.assignee_id = app.unit_head_id

ASSISTANT             app.stage = ModelLeaveCategory.SANCTION.value
ASSISTANT             app.assignee_id = app.user_id
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     app.save()

ASSISTANT     if accept:
ASSISTANT         new_status = "approved"  # Or a specific 'substitute_accepted' state if defined; using approved as per common practice

ASSISTANT         new_status = "pending"  # Revert to applicant action required state
ASSISTANT         description = "Substitute consent declined."
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     app.status = new_status
ASSISTANT     app.save()
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     log_action(app, actor_id, "substitute_response", description)
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     return app

ASSISTANT def withdraw(actor_id: int, application_id: int) -> LeaveApplication:
ASSISTANT     """Withdraw a pending leave request.
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     Only the owner may withdraw a PENDING request.
ASSISTANT     """

ASSISTANT

ASSISTANT         raise NotFound(f"Only the application owner can withdraw this application.")

ASSISTANT     app.status = "withdrawn"
ASSISTANT     app.save()
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     log_action(app, actor_id, "withdraw", f"Application withdrawn by user {actor_id}.")

USER Rules: no user table (people are integer user ids from request.principal.user_id); never import another module's models; domain/ is pure Python; all writes in services/ inside transaction.atomic; every state change appends a LeaveAction row.

USER ## models.py

USER - HalfDay(TextChoices): NONE="none", FIRST="first", SECOND="second".
USER - Stage(TextChoices): SUBSTITUTE="substitute", UNIT_HEAD="unit_head", ESTABLISHMENT="establishment", SANCTION="sanction", DONE="done".
USER - Status(TextChoices): PENDING="pending", APPROVED="approved", REJECTED="rejected", WITHDRAWN="withdrawn", CANCELLED="cancelled".
USER - SubstituteStatus(TextChoices): NOT_REQUIRED="not_required", PENDING="pending", ACCEPTED="accepted", DECLINED="declined".
USER - RequestKind(TextChoices): LEAVE="leave", CANCELLATION="cancellation".
USER - ReportingLine(TimeStampedModel): user_id IntegerField(unique=True), unit_head_id IntegerField, establishment_id IntegerField(null=True, blank=True), sanctioning_authority_id IntegerField(null=True, blank=True).
USER - LeaveApplication(UserScopedModel, TimeStampedModel): kind, parent ForeignKey("self", null=True, blank=True, on_delete=PROTECT, related_name="cancellations"), category, start_date, end_date, half_day (default NONE), days DecimalField(max_digits=5, decimal_places=1), purpose TextField, station_leave BooleanField(default=False), station_address TextField(blank=True), substitute_id IntegerField(null=True, blank=True), substitute_status (default NOT_REQUIRED), unit_head_id IntegerField, establishment_id IntegerField(null=True, blank=True), sanctioning_authority_id IntegerField(null=True, blank=True), unit_head_recommendation CharField(max_length=20, blank=True), stage, status (default PENDING), assignee_id IntegerField(null=True, blank=True, db_index=True), decided_at DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True). Meta ordering ["-created_at"].
USER - LeaveBalance(UserScopedModel, TimeStampedModel): category, year IntegerField, entitled DecimalField(5,1), used DecimalField(5,1, default=0). UniqueConstraint(user_id, year, category). property available = entitled - used.
USER - LeaveAction(models.Model): application ForeignKey(LeaveApplication, CASCADE, related_name="actions"), actor_id IntegerField, action CharField(40), remark TextField(blank=True), created_at DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True). Meta ordering ["created_at"].
USER 
USER ## domain/policy.py (pure Python, Decimal arithmetic)
USER - ENTITLEMENT = {category: (days_per_year, faculty_allowed, staff_allowed, carries_forward)}: CL (8, yes, yes, no) BR-EL-002; RH (2, yes, yes, no) BR-EL-003; SCL (15, yes, yes, no) BR-EL-004; EL (30, no, yes, yes) BR-EL-005; COL (20, yes, yes, yes) BR-EL-009; VL (60, yes, no, no) BR-EL-006.
USER - entitlement(category, kind) -> Decimal | None. kind "faculty" means faculty, anything else is staff. None means not eligible (BR-EL-001).
USER - count_days(category, start, end, half_day) -> Decimal. Raise PolicyError if end < start. half_day other than "none" only for CL and only when start == end, giving 0.5 (BR-EL-010). EL, COL, VL count every calendar day inclusive (BR-EL-011). CL, RH, SCL count Monday to Friday only (no holiday calendar yet).
USER - overlaps(a_start, a_end, a_half, b_start, b_end, b_half) -> bool. True when the date ranges intersect, except a single shared date where both halves are set and different (BR-EL-012).
USER - unit_head_is_final(category) -> bool: True for CL and RH (BR-EL-016); others only get a recommendation (BR-EL-017).
USER - class PolicyError(Exception).
USER 
USER ## Workflow (services)
USER apply -> stage SUBSTITUTE (assignee = substitute) if a substitute is nominated, else UNIT_HEAD (assignee = unit head).
USER Substitute accepts -> UNIT_HEAD. Declines -> substitute_status DECLINED, assignee None; applicant nominates a replacement (back to SUBSTITUTE) or withdraws (BR-EL-015).
USER UNIT_HEAD: CL/RH approve or reject is final. Other categories: "recommended" or "not_recommended" (not a rejection), then ESTABLISHMENT if establishment_id else SANCTION.
USER ESTABLISHMENT: verify -> SANCTION (assignee = sanctioning authority).
USER SANCTION: approve or reject is final.
USER If the applicant is their own unit head (unit_head_id == user_id, e.g. Director), skip UNIT_HEAD and go to SANCTION; if there is no sanctioning authority either, the applicant self-sanctions (BR-EL-020).
USER Approval of a LEAVE request adds days to LeaveBalance.used for the start year. Rejection or withdrawal changes no balance.
USER Withdraw: owner only, only while PENDING (BR-EL-022). Pending requests are never edited (BR-EL-034).
USER Cancellation: owner asks before start_date for an APPROVED leave; creates a CANCELLATION request (parent = the leave) routed like the original (BR-EL-023). Approving it marks the parent CANCELLED and subtracts its days from used.
USER Only the current assignee may act; anyone else gets NotFound (shown as 404, so ids are not leaked).
USER 
USER ## Permissions (registry.py, code "leave")
USER leave.application.apply, leave.application.view_own, leave.substitute.respond, leave.application.review, leave.application.verify, leave.application.sanction, leave.admin.manage.
USER Roles: Employee = apply, view_own, substitute.respond. Unit Head = review. Deputy Registrar (Estt.) = review, verify. Dean, Registrar, Director = review, sanction. Leave Administrator = admin.manage.
USER Nav: /leave/apply (apply), /leave/mine (view_own), /leave/substitute (substitute.respond), /leave/approvals (review).
USER 
USER ## API (base /api/v1/leave/)
USER GET/POST applications/ ; GET applications/<id>/ ; POST applications/<id>/withdraw/ ; POST applications/<id>/cancel/ ;
USER POST applications/<id>/substitute-response/ {"accept": bool} ; POST applications/<id>/replace-substitute/ {"substitute_id": int} ;
USER GET inbox/ ; POST applications/<id>/decide/ {"decision": str} ; GET balances/?year= ; GET/PUT reporting-lines/<user_id>/ (admin).
USER Errors reuse Fusion's core.api.exceptions: services raise LeaveError (class LeaveError(BadRequestError), HTTP 400) and NotFound (class NotFound(NotFoundError), HTTP 404); selectors raise core.api.exceptions.NotFoundError directly. The platform exception handler (core.api.exceptions.exception_handler) renders the standard {"error": {"code", "message", "details", "request_id"}} envelope, so views never catch these or build error responses themselves.

USER modules/leave/domain/policy.py
USER ```
USER """
USER Leave Policy Rules (BR-EL-001..015).
USER Pure Python implementation without Django/DRF/Celery imports.
USER """
USER 
USER from datetime import date
USER from decimal import Decimal, ROUND_HALF_UP
USER from typing import Dict, Optional
USER 
USER 
USER class PolicyError(Exception):
USER     """Domain policy error."""
USER     pass
USER 
USER 
USER CATEGORIES = ("CL", "RH", "SCL", "EL", "COL", "VL")
USER 
USER ENTITLEMENT: Dict[str, tuple] = {
USER     "CL": (Decimal("8"), Decimal("1"), Decimal("1"), Decimal("0")),  # BR-EL-002
USER     "RH": (Decimal("2"), Decimal("1"), Decimal("1"), Decimal("0")),  # BR-EL-003
USER     "SCL": (Decimal("15"), Decimal("1"), Decimal("1"), Decimal("0")),  # BR-EL-004
USER     "EL": (Decimal("30"), Decimal("0"), Decimal("1"), Decimal("1")),  # BR-EL-005
USER     "COL": (Decimal("20"), Decimal("1"), Decimal("1"), Decimal("1")),  # BR-EL-009
USER     "VL": (Decimal("60"), Decimal("1"), Decimal("0"), Decimal("0")),  # BR-EL-006
USER }
USER 
USER CONTINUOUS = {"EL", "COL", "VL"}
USER 
USER 
USER def entitlement(category: str, kind: str) -> Optional[Decimal]:
USER     """
USER     Return the annual leave entitlement for a category and kind.
USER     
USER     - kind="faculty" returns faculty allowance (or None if not eligible).
USER     - kind="staff" returns staff allowance (or None if not eligible).
USER     - Returns None if the category is not supported or the user kind is ineligible.
USER     
USER     BR-EL-001: Only supported categories allowed.
USER     BR-EL-005: EL is for non-faculty only (faculty_allowed=False).
USER     """
USER     if category not in CATEGORIES:
USER         raise PolicyError(f"Unknown leave category: {category}")  # BR-EL-001
USER     
USER     days_per_year, faculty_allowed, staff_allowed, carries_forward = ENTITLEMENT[category]
USER     
USER     if kind == "faculty":
USER         if faculty_allowed:
USER             return days_per_year
USER         else:
USER             return None  # EL is not for faculty
USER     elif kind == "staff":
USER         if staff_allowed:
USER             return days_per_year
USER         else:
USER             return None  # VL is not for staff (faculty_allowed=True, staff_allowed=False)
USER     
USER     return None
USER 
USER 
USER def count_days(category: str, start: date, end: date, half_day: str = "none") -> Decimal:
USER     """
USER     Calculate the number of leave days consumed.
USER     
USER     BR-EL-010: Half-day CL consumes 0.5 day (only for CL and when start==end).
USER     BR-EL-011: EL, COL, VL count every calendar day inclusive (weekends/holidays included).
USER                CL, RH, SCL count Monday to Friday only (no holiday calendar yet -> assume working days).
USER     
USER     Note: Since no external calendar service is imported, we assume the input dates represent
USER     the requested interval. For CL/RH/SCL, we will perform a basic weekday check assuming
USER     standard work weeks (Mon-Fri) as per BR-EL-011 implication for non-continuous categories.
USER     """
USER     if start > end:
USER         raise PolicyError("End date must be on or after start date")  # Generic validation
USER     
USER     if half_day != "none" and category not in ("CL",):
USER         raise PolicyError(f"Half-day consumption only applies to CL. Category: {category}")  # BR-EL-010
USER     
USER     if half_day != "none" and start != end:
USER         raise PolicyError("Half-day consumption only allowed for single-day requests")  # BR-EL-010
USER     
USER     days = Decimal(str((end - start).days + 1))
USER     
USER     category_upper = category.upper()
USER     
USER     if category_upper in CONTINUOUS:
USER         # BR-EL-011: EL, COL, VL count every calendar day inclusive.
USER         # No filtering for weekends/holidays.
USER         pass
USER     else:
USER         # CL, RH, SCL: Count Monday to Friday only.
USER         # Since no holiday calendar is available, we assume standard working days (Mon-Fri).
USER         current = start
USER         while current <= end:
USER             if current.weekday() < 5:  # Monday=0 to Friday=4
USER                 days += Decimal("1")
USER             current += date(1)
USER     
USER     if half_day != "none":
USER         # Apply half-day consumption (0.5 day)
USER         days *= Decimal("0.5")
USER     
USER     return days
USER 
USER 
USER def overlaps(a_start: date, a_end: date, a_half: str, b_start: date, b_end: date, b_half: str) -> bool:
USER     """
USER     Check if two leave requests overlap.
USER     
USER     True when the date ranges intersect, except a single shared date where both halves are set and different.
USER     BR-EL-012: Applicant non-overlap with half-day CL exception.
USER     """
USER     # Determine if ranges intersect
USER     # Intersection exists if: start_a <= end_b AND start_b <= end_a
USER     intersects = (a_start <= b_end) and (b_start <= a_end)
USER     
USER     if not intersects:
USER         return False
USER     
USER     # Check for the half-day exception on the same date
USER     # Exception applies only to CL (implied by context of BR-EL-012 regarding halves)
USER     # If both are on the same day and have different halves, they do NOT overlap.
USER     if a_start == b_start:
USER         if a_half != "none" and b_half != "none":
USER             # Both are half-days on the same day
USER             if a_half != b_half:
USER                 return False  # Different halves on same date -> non-overlapping
USER     
USER     return True
USER 
USER 
USER def unit_head_is_final(category: str) -> bool:
USER     """
USER     Determine if Unit Head has final decision power.
USER     
USER     BR-EL-016: True for CL and RH.
USER     BR-EL-017: Others only get a recommendation.
USER     """
USER     return category in ("CL", "RH")
USER ```

ASSISTANT Ok, any changes I propose will be to those files.

USER Review of policy.py from task 002 found these defects. Fix only these, with SEARCH/REPLACE edits:
USER 
USER 1. count_days() is wrong for CL, RH and SCL. It sets days to the total calendar days and then ADDS one for every weekday again, so Monday-Tuesday gives 4 instead of 2. It also advances the loop with `current += date(1)`, which raises TypeError. Fix: for categories not in CONTINUOUS, start days at Decimal("0") and count only dates with weekday() < 5, advancing with `current += timedelta(days=1)` (import timedelta from datetime). For CONTINUOUS categories keep days = (end - start).days + 1.
USER 2. For a half-day request (half_day != "none"), return Decimal("0.5") directly (BR-EL-010) instead of multiplying.
USER 3. In ENTITLEMENT, the faculty_allowed, staff_allowed and carries_forward values must be the booleans True/False, not Decimal("1")/Decimal("0").
USER 4. Remove the unused ROUND_HALF_UP import.
USER 5. entitlement() returns None for any kind other than "faculty" or "staff", so an applicant whose kind is "employee" or "" gets no leave. Per DESIGN.md, kind "faculty" means faculty and ANY other kind means staff: replace the `elif kind == "staff":` branch with an `else:` branch that uses staff_allowed, and remove the final `return None`.
USER 
USER Do not change anything else.
USER

ASSISTANT def count_days(category: str, start: date, end: date, half_day: str = "none") -> Decimal:
ASSISTANT     """
ASSISTANT     Calculate the number of leave days consumed.
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     BR-EL-010: Half-day CL consumes 0.5 day (only for CL and when start==end).
ASSISTANT     BR-EL-011: EL, COL, VL count every calendar day inclusive (weekends/holidays included).
ASSISTANT                CL, RH, SCL count Monday to Friday only (no holiday calendar yet -> assume working days).
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     Note: Since no external calendar service is imported, we assume the input dates represent
ASSISTANT     the requested interval. For CL/RH/SCL, we will perform a basic weekday check assuming
ASSISTANT     standard work weeks (Mon-Fri) as per BR-EL-011 implication for non-continuous categories.
ASSISTANT     """
ASSISTANT     if start > end:
ASSISTANT         raise PolicyError("End date must be on or after start date")  # Generic validation
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     if half_day != "none" and category not in ("CL",):
ASSISTANT         raise PolicyError(f"Half-day consumption only applies to CL. Category: {category}")  # BR-EL-010
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     if half_day != "none" and start != end:
ASSISTANT         raise PolicyError("Half-day consumption only allowed for single-day requests")  # BR-EL-010
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     days = Decimal(str((end - start).days + 1))
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     category_upper = category.upper()
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     if category_upper in CONTINUOUS:
ASSISTANT         # BR-EL-011: EL, COL, VL count every calendar day inclusive.
ASSISTANT         # No filtering for weekends/holidays.
ASSISTANT         pass
ASSISTANT     else:
ASSISTANT         # CL, RH, SCL: Count Monday to Friday only.
ASSISTANT         # Since no holiday calendar is available, we assume standard working days (Mon-Fri).
ASSISTANT         current = start
ASSISTANT         while current <= end:
ASSISTANT             if current.weekday() < 5:  # Monday=0 to Friday=4
ASSISTANT                 days += Decimal("1")
ASSISTANT             current += date(1)
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     if half_day != "none":
ASSISTANT         # Apply half-day consumption (0.5 day)
ASSISTANT         days *= Decimal("0.5")
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     return days

ASSISTANT def entitlement(category: str, kind: str) -> Optional[Decimal]:
ASSISTANT     """
ASSISTANT     Return the annual leave entitlement for a category and kind.
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     - kind="faculty" returns faculty allowance (or None if not eligible).
ASSISTANT     - kind="staff" returns staff allowance (or None if not eligible).
ASSISTANT     - Returns None if the category is not supported or the user kind is ineligible.
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     BR-EL-001: Only supported categories allowed.
ASSISTANT     BR-EL-005: EL is for non-faculty only (faculty_allowed=False).
ASSISTANT     """
ASSISTANT     if category not in CATEGORIES:
ASSISTANT         raise PolicyError(f"Unknown leave category: {category}")  # BR-EL-001
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     days_per_year, faculty_allowed, staff_allowed, carries_forward = ENTITLEMENT[category]
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     if kind == "faculty":
ASSISTANT         if faculty_allowed:
ASSISTANT             return days_per_year
ASSISTANT         else:
ASSISTANT             return None  # EL is not for faculty
ASSISTANT     elif kind == "staff":
ASSISTANT         if staff_allowed:
ASSISTANT             return days_per_year
ASSISTANT         else:
ASSISTANT             return None  # VL is not for staff (faculty_allowed=True, staff_allowed=False)
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     return None

USER """
USER Leave Policy Rules (BR-EL-001..015).
USER Pure Python implementation without Django/DRF/Celery imports.
USER """
USER 
USER from datetime import date, timedelta
USER from decimal import Decimal
USER from typing import Dict, Optional
USER 
USER 
USER class PolicyError(Exception):
USER     """Domain policy error."""
USER     pass
USER 
USER 
USER CATEGORIES = ("CL", "RH", "SCL", "EL", "COL", "VL")
USER 
USER ENTITLEMENT: Dict[str, tuple] = {
USER     "CL": (Decimal("8"), True, True, False),  # BR-EL-002
USER     "RH": (Decimal("2"), True, True, False),  # BR-EL-003
USER     "SCL": (Decimal("15"), True, True, False),  # BR-EL-004
USER     "EL": (Decimal("30"), False, True, True),  # BR-EL-005
USER     "COL": (Decimal("20"), True, True, True),  # BR-EL-009
USER     "VL": (Decimal("60"), True, False, False),  # BR-EL-006
USER }
USER 
USER CONTINUOUS = {"EL", "COL", "VL"}
USER 
USER 
USER def entitlement(category: str, kind: str) -> Optional[Decimal]:
USER     """
USER     Return the annual leave entitlement for a category and kind.
USER     
USER     - kind="faculty" returns faculty allowance (or None if not eligible).
USER     - Any other kind (e.g., "staff", "employee", "") is treated as staff.
USER     - Returns None if the category is not supported or the user kind is ineligible.
USER     
USER     BR-EL-001: Only supported categories allowed.
USER     BR-EL-005: EL is for non-faculty only (faculty_allowed=False).
USER     """
USER     if category not in CATEGORIES:
USER         raise PolicyError(f"Unknown leave category: {category}")  # BR-EL-001
USER     
USER     days_per_year, faculty_allowed, staff_allowed, carries_forward = ENTITLEMENT[category]
USER     
USER     is_faculty = kind == "faculty"
USER     
USER     if is_faculty:
USER         if faculty_allowed:
USER             return days_per_year
USER         else:
USER             return None  # EL is not for faculty
USER     else:
USER         # Any kind other than "faculty" is treated as staff
USER         if staff_allowed:
USER             return days_per_year
USER         else:
USER             return None  # VL is not for staff (faculty_allowed=True, staff_allowed=False)
USER 
USER 
USER def count_days(category: str, start: date, end: date, half_day: str = "none") -> Decimal:
USER     """
USER     Calculate the number of leave days consumed.
USER     
USER     BR-EL-010: Half-day CL consumes 0.5 day (only for CL and when start==end).
USER     BR-EL-011: EL, COL, VL count every calendar day inclusive (weekends/holidays included).
USER                CL, RH, SCL count Monday to Friday only (no holiday calendar yet -> assume working days).
USER     
USER     Note: Since no external calendar service is imported, we assume the input dates represent
USER     the requested interval. For CL/RH/SCL, we will perform a basic weekday check assuming
USER     standard work weeks (Mon-Fri) as per BR-EL-011 implication for non-continuous categories.
USER     """
USER     if start > end:
USER         raise PolicyError("End date must be on or after start date")  # Generic validation
USER     
USER     if half_day != "none" and category not in ("CL",):
USER         raise PolicyError(f"Half-day consumption only applies to CL. Category: {category}")  # BR-EL-010
USER     
USER     if half_day != "none" and start != end:
USER         raise PolicyError("Half-day consumption only allowed for single-day requests")  # BR-EL-010
USER     
USER     category_upper = category.upper()
USER     
USER     if category_upper in CONTINUOUS:
USER         # BR-EL-011: EL, COL, VL count every calendar day inclusive.
USER         # No filtering for weekends/holidays.
USER         days = Decimal(str((end - start).days + 1))
USER     else:
USER         # CL, RH, SCL: Count Monday to Friday only.
USER         # Since no holiday calendar is available, we assume standard working days (Mon-Fri).
USER         days = Decimal("0")
USER         current = start
USER         while current <= end:
USER             if current.weekday() < 5:  # Monday=0 to Friday=4
USER                 days += Decimal("1")
USER             current += timedelta(days=1)
USER     
USER     if half_day != "none":
USER         # Apply half-day consumption (0.5 day) directly per BR-EL-010
USER         return Decimal("0.5")
USER     
USER     return days
USER 
USER 
USER def overlaps(a_start: date, a_end: date, a_half: str, b_start: date, b_end: date, b_half: str) -> bool:
USER     """
USER     Check if two leave requests overlap.
USER     
USER     True when the date ranges intersect, except a single shared date where both halves are set and different.
USER     BR-EL-012: Applicant non-overlap with half-day CL exception.
USER     """
USER     # Determine if ranges intersect
USER     # Intersection exists if: start_a <= end_b AND start_b <= end_a
USER     intersects = (a_start <= b_end) and (b_start <= a_end)
USER     
USER     if not intersects:
USER         return False
USER     
USER     # Check for the half-day exception on the same date
USER     # Exception applies only to CL (implied by context of BR-EL-012 regarding halves)
USER     # If both are on the same day and have different halves, they do NOT overlap.
USER     if a_start == b_start:
USER         if a_half != "none" and b_half != "none":
USER             # Both are half-days on the same day
USER             if a_half != b_half:
USER                 return False  # Different halves on same date -> non-overlapping
USER     
USER     return True
USER 
USER 
USER def unit_head_is_final(category: str) -> bool:
USER     """
USER     Determine if Unit Head has final decision power.
USER     
USER     BR-EL-016: True for CL and RH.
USER     BR-EL-017: Others only get a recommendation.
USER     """
USER     return category in ("CL", "RH")

USER ## domain/policy.py (pure Python, Decimal arithmetic)
USER - ENTITLEMENT = {category: (days_per_year, faculty_allowed, staff_allowed, carries_forward)}: CL (8, yes, yes, no) BR-EL-002; RH (2, yes, yes, no) BR-EL-003; SCL (15, yes, yes, no) BR-EL-004; EL (30, no, yes, yes) BR-EL-005; COL (20, yes, yes, yes) BR-EL-009; VL (60, yes, no, no) BR-EL-006.
USER - entitlement(category, kind) -> Decimal | None. kind "faculty" means faculty, anything else is staff. None means not eligible (BR-EL-001).
USER - count_days(category, start, end, half_day) -> Decimal. Raise PolicyError if end < start. half_day other than "none" only for CL and only when start == end, giving 0.5 (BR-EL-010). EL, COL, VL count every calendar day inclusive (BR-EL-011). CL, RH, SCL count Monday to Friday only (no holiday calendar yet).
USER - overlaps(a_start, a_end, a_half, b_start, b_end, b_half) -> bool. True when the date ranges intersect, except a single shared date where both halves are set and different (BR-EL-012).
USER - unit_head_is_final(category) -> bool: True for CL and RH (BR-EL-016); others only get a recommendation (BR-EL-017).
USER - class PolicyError(Exception).
USER 
USER ## Workflow (services)
USER apply -> stage SUBSTITUTE (assignee = substitute) if a substitute is nominated, else UNIT_HEAD (assignee = unit head).
USER Substitute accepts -> UNIT_HEAD. Declines -> substitute_status DECLINED, assignee None; applicant nominates a replacement (back to SUBSTITUTE) or withdraws (BR-EL-015).
USER UNIT_HEAD: CL/RH approve or reject is final. Other categories: "recommended" or "not_recommended" (not a rejection), then ESTABLISHMENT if establishment_id else SANCTION.
USER ESTABLISHMENT: verify -> SANCTION (assignee = sanctioning authority).
USER SANCTION: approve or reject is final.
USER If the applicant is their own unit head (unit_head_id == user_id, e.g. Director), skip UNIT_HEAD and go to SANCTION; if there is no sanctioning authority either, the applicant self-sanctions (BR-EL-020).
USER Approval of a LEAVE request adds days to LeaveBalance.used for the start year. Rejection or withdrawal changes no balance.
USER Withdraw: owner only, only while PENDING (BR-EL-022). Pending requests are never edited (BR-EL-034).
USER Cancellation: owner asks before start_date for an APPROVED leave; creates a CANCELLATION request (parent = the leave) routed like the original (BR-EL-023). Approving it marks the parent CANCELLED and subtracts its days from used.
USER Only the current assignee may act; anyone else gets NotFound (shown as 404, so ids are not leaked).
USER 
USER ## Permissions (registry.py, code "leave")
USER leave.application.apply, leave.application.view_own, leave.substitute.respond, leave.application.review, leave.application.verify, leave.application.sanction, leave.admin.manage.
USER Roles: Employee = apply, view_own, substitute.respond. Unit Head = review. Deputy Registrar (Estt.) = review, verify. Dean, Registrar, Director = review, sanction. Leave Administrator = admin.manage.
USER Nav: /leave/apply (apply), /leave/mine (view_own), /leave/substitute (substitute.respond), /leave/approvals (review).
USER 
USER ## API (base /api/v1/leave/)
USER GET/POST applications/ ; GET applications/<id>/ ; POST applications/<id>/withdraw/ ; POST applications/<id>/cancel/ ;
USER POST applications/<id>/substitute-response/ {"accept": bool} ; POST applications/<id>/replace-substitute/ {"substitute_id": int} ;
USER GET inbox/ ; POST applications/<id>/decide/ {"decision": str} ; GET balances/?year= ; GET/PUT reporting-lines/<user_id>/ (admin).
USER Errors reuse Fusion's core.api.exceptions: services raise LeaveError (class LeaveError(BadRequestError), HTTP 400) and NotFound (class NotFound(NotFoundError), HTTP 404); selectors raise core.api.exceptions.NotFoundError directly. The platform exception handler (core.api.exceptions.exception_handler) renders the standard {"error": {"code", "message", "details", "request_id"}} envelope, so views never catch these or build error responses themselves.
