USER """
USER Clean implementation of leave application workflow based on DESIGN.md.
USER Implements eligibility checks, overlap detection, balance validation, and routing logic.
USER All writes are wrapped in transactions and history is logged via LeaveAction.
USER """
USER 
USER from datetime import date
USER from decimal import Decimal
USER from typing import Any, Optional
USER 
USER from django.db import transaction, models
USER 
USER from modules.leave.domain.policy import (
USER     PolicyError,
USER     calculate_consumed_days,
USER     check_applicant_overlap,
USER     requires_substitute,
USER     unit_head_decision_required,
USER     get_categories_requiring_unit_head_decision,
USER     ENTITLEMENT,
USER )
USER from modules.leave.models import (
USER     LeaveApplication,
USER     LeaveBalance,
USER     LeaveAction,
USER     ReportingLine,
USER     LeaveCategory as ModelLeaveCategory,
USER )
USER 
USER 
USER class LeaveError(Exception):
USER     """Domain error for leave operations."""
USER     pass
USER 
USER 
USER class NotFound(LeaveError):
USER     """Resource not found."""
USER     pass
USER 
USER 
USER def log_action(app: LeaveApplication, actor_id: int, action: str, remark: str = "") -> None:
USER     """Append an entry to the leave action history."""
USER     LeaveAction.objects.create(
USER         application=app,
USER         actor_id=actor_id,
USER         action=action,
USER         remark=remark,
USER     )
USER 
USER 
USER def get_balance(user_id: int, kind: str, category: str, year: int) -> LeaveBalance:
USER     """Get or create leave balance for a user.
USER 
USER     Raises LeaveError if the user is not eligible for the category/kind combination.
USER     """
USER     try:
USER         entitlement = PolicyError.__bases__[0].__subclasses__()[0].entitlement(category, kind)  # type: ignore
USER     except PolicyError:
USER         raise LeaveError(f"Not eligible for {category} leave as {kind}.")
USER 
USER     balance, created = LeaveBalance.objects.get_or_create(
USER         user_id=user_id,
USER         year=year,
USER         category=category,
USER         defaults={
USER             "entitled": Decimal(str(entitlement)),
USER             "used": Decimal("0"),
USER         },
USER     )
USER 
USER     if not created:
USER         # Ensure entitled matches policy (in case of manual edits or data sync issues)
USER         balance.entitled = Decimal(str(entitlement))
USER         balance.save()
USER 
USER     return balance
USER 
USER 
USER def has_overlap(user_id: int, start: date, end: date, half_day: str, exclude_id: Optional[int] = None) -> bool:
USER     """Check if the user has an overlapping leave application.
USER 
USER     Considers PENDING or APPROVED LEAVE-kind applications.
USER     Applies BR-EL-012 exception for CL half-days on single dates.
USER     """
USER     # Determine which categories count as "LEAVE" for overlap purposes (excluding CANCELLATION)
USER     leave_categories = [cat for cat in ModelLeaveCategory.choices if cat[0] != "CANCELLATION"]
USER 
USER     applications = LeaveApplication.objects.filter(
USER         user_id=user_id,
USER         category__in=leave_categories,
USER         status__in=["pending", "approved"],
USER     )
USER 
USER     # Exclude the current application if provided
USER     if exclude_id is not None:
USER         applications = applications.exclude(id=exclude_id)
USER 
USER     for app in applications:
USER         # Calculate overlap using policy logic
USER         # Note: The policy module's overlaps function expects date objects and handles half-day logic.
USER         # We need to ensure the dates are comparable.
USER         try:
USER             is_overlapping = PolicyError.__bases__[0].__subclasses__()[0].overlaps(  # type: ignore
USER                 app.start_date, app.end_date, app.half_day, start, end, half_day
USER             )
USER             if is_overlapping:
USER                 return True
USER         except Exception:
USER             # Fallback in case policy module logic changes or raises unexpected errors
USER             continue
USER 
USER     return False
USER 
USER 
USER def route_to_approver(app: LeaveApplication) -> None:
USER     """Route the application to the appropriate approver based on hierarchy.
USER 
USER     - If unit_head_id == user_id (self-sanctioning authority): go to SANCTION.
USER     - If no sanctioning_authority_id: self-sanction (SANCTION).
USER     - Otherwise: route to UNIT_HEAD first, then ESTABLISHMENT, then SANCTION.
USER     """
USER     # Determine the initial assignee based on category and hierarchy
USER     if requires_substitute(ModelLeaveCategory(app.category)):
USER         # If a substitute is nominated, route to SUBSTITUTE stage
USER         app.stage = ModelLeaveCategory.SUBSTITUTE.value  # Assuming SUBSTITUTE is a valid Stage choice
USER         app.assignee_id = app.substitute_id
USER         app.substitute_status = "pending"
USER     else:
USER         # Default routing logic for non-substitute categories
USER         if app.unit_head_id == app.user_id:
USER             # Self-sanctioning authority (e.g., Director)
USER             app.stage = ModelLeaveCategory.SANCTION.value
USER             app.assignee_id = app.user_id
USER         elif app.sanctioning_authority_id is not None:
USER             # Standard hierarchy: Unit Head -> Establishment -> Sanctioning Authority
USER             # Per DESIGN.md, we route to UNIT_HEAD first for review/recommendation
USER             app.stage = ModelLeaveCategory.UNIT_HEAD.value
USER             app.assignee_id = app.unit_head_id
USER         else:
USER             # Fallback: if no unit head or sanctioning authority configured, default to user (shouldn't happen in valid data)
USER             app.stage = ModelLeaveCategory.SANCTION.value
USER             app.assignee_id = app.user_id
USER 
USER     app.save()
USER 
USER 
USER def apply_leave(user_id: int, kind: str, data: dict) -> LeaveApplication:
USER     """Create and submit a new leave application.
USER 
USER     Validates eligibility, balance, overlaps, and substitutes.
USER     Routes the application to the appropriate approver.
USER     """
USER     category = data["category"]
USER     start_date = data["start_date"]
USER     end_date = data["end_date"]
USER     half_day = data.get("half_day", "none")
USER     purpose = data["purpose"]
USER     station_leave = data.get("station_leave", False)
USER     station_address = data.get("station_address", "")
USER     substitute_id = data.get("substitute_id")
USER 
USER     # 1. Check eligibility (BR-EL-001, BR-EL-005, etc.)
USER     try:
USER         entitlement = PolicyError.__bases__[0].__subclasses__()[0].entitlement(category, kind)  # type: ignore
USER     except PolicyError as e:
USER         raise LeaveError(str(e))
USER 
USER     if entitlement is None:
USER         raise LeaveError(f"Not eligible for {category} leave as {kind}.")
USER 
USER     # 2. Compute days (BR-EL-010, BR-EL-011)
USER     try:
USER         days = PolicyError.__bases__[0].__subclasses__()[0].count_days(category, start_date, end_date, half_day)  # type: ignore
USER     except PolicyError as e:
USER         raise LeaveError(str(e))
USER 
USER     if days <= 0:
USER         raise LeaveError(f"Invalid leave duration: {days} days.")
USER 
USER     # 3. Check balance (BR-EL-025)
USER     try:
USER         balance = get_balance(user_id, kind, category, start_date.year)
USER     except LeaveError as e:
USER         raise LeaveError(str(e))
USER 
USER     if balance.available < days:
USER         raise LeaveError(f"Insufficient leave balance. Available: {balance.available}, Required: {days}.")
USER 
USER     # 4. Check overlap (BR-EL-012)
USER     if has_overlap(user_id, start_date, end_date, half_day):
USER         raise LeaveError("Leave application overlaps with existing pending or approved leave.")
USER 
USER     # 5. Handle substitute logic (BR-EL-014)
USER     if requires_substitute(ModelLeaveCategory(category)):
USER         if substitute_id is None:
USER             raise LeaveError("Substitute must be nominated for this category.")
USER 
USER         # Check if substitute is the applicant
USER         if substitute_id == user_id:
USER             raise LeaveError("Substitute cannot be the applicant.")
USER 
USER         # Check if substitute has overlapping leave
USER         try:
USER             sub_balance = get_balance(substitute_id, kind, category, start_date.year)
USER             # We need to check overlap for the substitute too. 
USER             # Simplified: assume we check overlap for the substitute's pending/approved leaves.
USER             # Since we don't have a direct 'has_overlap' function that takes another user_id easily without modifying scope,
USER             # we'll rely on the fact that the policy module's overlaps function checks against a specific user.
USER             # Let's assume the substitute's overlap check is done similarly or via a helper if needed.
USER             # For now, we'll proceed assuming the substitute is eligible unless explicitly checked elsewhere.
USER             pass
USER         except Exception:
USER             pass
USER 
USER         # Check for existing pending/approved leave of the substitute that overlaps
USER         # We need to implement a quick check here similar to has_overlap but for the substitute
USER         from modules.leave.models import LeaveApplication as LA
USER         
USER         sub_apps = LA.objects.filter(
USER             user_id=substitute_id,
USER             category=category,
USER             status__in=["pending", "approved"],
USER         )
USER 
USER         for sub_app in sub_apps:
USER             try:
USER                 is_overlapping = PolicyError.__bases__[0].__subclasses__()[0].overlaps(  # type: ignore
USER                     sub_app.start_date, sub_app.end_date, sub_app.half_day, start_date, end_date, half_day
USER                 )
USER                 if is_overlapping:
USER                     raise LeaveError(f"Substitute {substitute_id} has an overlapping leave application.")
USER             except Exception:
USER                 continue
USER 
USER     # 6. Load ReportingLine (BR-EL-018)
USER     try:
USER         reporting_line = ReportingLine.objects.get(user_id=user_id)
USER     except ReportingLine.DoesNotExist:
USER         raise LeaveError("No reporting line configured.")
USER 
USER     # 7. Copy hierarchy IDs from ReportingLine to application
USER     app, created = LeaveApplication.objects.get_or_create(
USER         user_id=user_id,
USER         defaults={
USER             "category": category,
USER             "start_date": start_date,
USER             "end_date": end_date,
USER             "half_day": half_day,
USER             "purpose": purpose,
USER             "station_leave": station_leave,
USER             "station_address": station_address,
USER             "substitute_id": substitute_id,
USER             "unit_head_id": reporting_line.unit_head_id,
USER             "establishment_id": reporting_line.establishment_id,
USER             "sanctioning_authority_id": reporting_line.sanctioning_authority_id,
USER         },
USER     )
USER 
USER     if not created:
USER         # Update fields if they changed (though typically this is a new application)
USER         app.category = category
USER         app.start_date = start_date
USER         app.end_date = end_date
USER         app.half_day = half_day
USER         app.purpose = purpose
USER         app.station_leave = station_leave
USER         app.station_address = station_address
USER         app.substitute_id = substitute_id
USER         app.unit_head_id = reporting_line.unit_head_id
USER         app.establishment_id = reporting_line.establishment_id
USER         app.sanctioning_authority_id = reporting_line.sanctioning_authority_id
USER         app.save()
USER 
USER     # 8. Route to approver (BR-EL-015, BR-EL-020)
USER     route_to_approver(app)
USER 
USER     # Log action
USER     log_action(app, user_id, "apply", f"Leave application submitted for {category} leave.")
USER 
USER     return app
USER 
USER 
USER def respond_to_substitute(actor_id: int, application_id: int, accept: bool) -> LeaveApplication:
USER     """Accept or decline a substitute request.
USER 
USER     Only the current assignee (substitute) may respond.
USER     """
USER     app = LeaveApplication.objects.get(id=application_id)
USER 
USER     if app.assignee_id != actor_id:
USER         raise NotFound(f"Only the assigned substitute can respond to this application.")
USER 
USER     if app.status not in ["pending", "submitted"]:  # Assuming 'pending' or 'submitted' states allow response
USER         raise NotFound(f"Cannot respond to substitute request for application in status {app.status}.")
USER 
USER     old_status = app.status
USER     if accept:
USER         new_status = "approved"  # Or a specific 'substitute_accepted' state if defined; using approved as per common practice
USER         description = "Substitute consent accepted."
USER     else:
USER         new_status = "pending"  # Revert to applicant action required state
USER         description = "Substitute consent declined."
USER 
USER     app.status = new_status
USER     app.save()
USER 
USER     log_action(app, actor_id, "substitute_response", description)
USER 
USER     return app
USER 
USER 
USER def replace_substitute(actor_id: int, application_id: int, substitute_id: int) -> LeaveApplication:
USER     """Nominate a replacement substitute after a decline.
USER 
USER     Only the owner (applicant) may replace a declined substitute.
USER     """
USER     app = LeaveApplication.objects.get(id=application_id)
USER 
USER     if app.user_id != actor_id:
USER         raise NotFound(f"Only the application owner can nominate a replacement substitute.")
USER 
USER     if app.substitute_status != "declined":
USER         raise NotFound(f"Can only replace a declined substitute. Current status: {app.substitute_status}.")
USER 
USER     # Validate new substitute eligibility (simplified: not applicant, no overlap)
USER     if substitute_id == actor_id:
USER         raise LeaveError("Substitute cannot be the applicant.")
USER 
USER     # Check for existing pending/approved leave of the new substitute that overlaps
USER     from modules.leave.models import LeaveApplication as LA
USER     
USER     sub_apps = LA.objects.filter(
USER         user_id=substitute_id,
USER         category=app.category,
USER         status__in=["pending", "approved"],
USER     )
USER 
USER     for sub_app in sub_apps:
USER         try:
USER             is_overlapping = PolicyError.__bases__[0].__subclasses__()[0].overlaps(  # type: ignore
USER                 sub_app.start_date, sub_app.end_date, sub_app.half_day, app.start_date, app.end_date, app.half_day
USER             )
USER             if is_overlapping:
USER                 raise LeaveError(f"New substitute {substitute_id} has an overlapping leave application.")
USER         except Exception:
USER             continue
USER 
USER     # Update the nominated substitute ID and status
USER     app.substitute_id = substitute_id
USER     app.substitute_status = "pending"  # Reset to pending for new nomination
USER     app.save()
USER 
USER     log_action(app, actor_id, "replace_substitute", f"New substitute {substitute_id} nominated.")
USER 
USER     return app
USER 
USER 
USER def withdraw(actor_id: int, application_id: int) -> LeaveApplication:
USER     """Withdraw a pending leave request.
USER 
USER     Only the owner may withdraw a PENDING request.
USER     """
USER     app = LeaveApplication.objects.get(id=application_id)
USER 
USER     if app.user_id != actor_id:
USER         raise NotFound(f"Only the application owner can withdraw this application.")
USER 
USER     if app.status not in ["pending", "submitted"]:  # Assuming 'pending' or 'submitted' states allow withdrawal before final approval
USER         raise NotFound(f"Cannot withdraw application in status {app.status}.")
USER 
USER     old_status = app.status
USER     app.status = "withdrawn"
USER     app.save()
USER 
USER     log_action(app, actor_id, "withdraw", f"Application withdrawn by user {actor_id}.")
USER 
USER     return app

ASSISTANT Ok, any changes I propose will be to those files.
-------
USER Review of modules/leave/services/applications.py from task 003 found these defects (the file does not even import):
USER 1. It imports names that do not exist in policy.py (calculate_consumed_days, check_applicant_overlap, requires_substitute, unit_head_decision_required, get_categories_requiring_unit_head_decision). policy.py only has: PolicyError, CATEGORIES, ENTITLEMENT, entitlement, count_days, overlaps, unit_head_is_final.
USER 2. It calls policy functions through `PolicyError.__bases__[0].__subclasses__()[0]...`. Call them directly: policy.entitlement(...), policy.count_days(...), policy.overlaps(...).
USER 3. apply_leave uses LeaveApplication.objects.get_or_create(user_id=...), which overwrites the applicant's previous application. Every apply must create a NEW row with LeaveApplication.objects.create(...), including kind=RequestKind.LEAVE and days.
USER 4. `except Exception: continue` around the overlap checks swallows the LeaveError it raises, so overlaps are never rejected. Do not wrap the overlap checks in try/except.
USER 5. A substitute is optional (DESIGN.md): there is no "requires substitute" rule. If data["substitute_id"] is not None: reject it when it equals the applicant, reject it when the substitute has a PENDING or APPROVED LEAVE-kind application of ANY category overlapping the dates, then set stage=Stage.SUBSTITUTE, assignee_id=substitute_id, substitute_status=SubstituteStatus.PENDING. Otherwise substitute_status=SubstituteStatus.NOT_REQUIRED and call route_to_approver(app).
USER 6. route_to_approver uses LeaveCategory.SUBSTITUTE / SANCTION, which do not exist. Use Stage. Rule: if app.unit_head_id == app.user_id then stage=Stage.SANCTION and assignee_id = app.sanctioning_authority_id or app.user_id (self-sanction, BR-EL-020); else stage=Stage.UNIT_HEAD and assignee_id=app.unit_head_id. It must not look at substitutes.
USER 7. respond_to_substitute sets status "approved" on accept. Correct: only when stage is SUBSTITUTE, status PENDING and assignee_id == actor_id (else NotFound). Accept: substitute_status=ACCEPTED then route_to_approver(app). Decline: substitute_status=DECLINED, assignee_id=None, status stays PENDING.
USER 8. replace_substitute: only the owner, only while status is PENDING and substitute_status is DECLINED (else NotFound); validate the new substitute as in item 5; set substitute_id, substitute_status=PENDING, stage=Stage.SUBSTITUTE, assignee_id=new substitute.
USER 9. withdraw: only the owner and only status PENDING (else NotFound); set status=Status.WITHDRAWN, assignee_id=None.
USER 10. Status strings such as "submitted" do not exist. Use the TextChoices from models: Status.PENDING, Status.APPROVED, Status.WITHDRAWN, Stage.*, SubstituteStatus.*, RequestKind.LEAVE.
USER 11. has_overlap must filter kind=RequestKind.LEAVE and status in (PENDING, APPROVED), not category__in.
USER 12. Nothing is wrapped in transaction.atomic. Decorate apply_leave, respond_to_substitute, replace_substitute and withdraw with @transaction.atomic and load existing rows with LeaveApplication.objects.select_for_update().filter(id=application_id).first() (None -> NotFound).

ASSISTANT def respond_to_substitute(actor_id: int, application_id: int, accept: bool) -> LeaveApplication:
ASSISTANT     """Accept or decline a substitute request.
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     Only the current assignee (substitute) may respond.
ASSISTANT     """
ASSISTANT     app = LeaveApplication.objects.select_for_update().get(id=application_id)
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     if app.assignee_id != actor_id:
ASSISTANT         raise NotFound(f"Only the assigned substitute can respond to this application.")
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     if app.status not in [Status.PENDING.value, Stage.SUBSTITUTE.value]:  # Assuming 'pending' or 'submitted' states allow response
ASSISTANT         raise NotFound(f"Cannot respond to substitute request for application in status {app.status}.")
ASSISTANT

ASSISTANT     if accept:
ASSISTANT         new_status = Status.PENDING.value  # Keep pending but mark substitute accepted
ASSISTANT         description = "Substitute consent accepted."
ASSISTANT         app.substitute_status = SubstituteStatus.ACCEPTED.value
ASSISTANT         # Route to approver after acceptance
ASSISTANT         route_to_approver(app)
ASSISTANT     else:
ASSISTANT         new_status = Status.PENDING.value  # Revert to applicant action required state
ASSISTANT         description = "Substitute consent declined."
ASSISTANT         app.substitute_status = SubstituteStatus.DECLINED.value
ASSISTANT         app.assignee_id = None
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     app.save()
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     log_action(app, actor_id, "substitute_response", description)
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     return app

USER modules/leave/domain/policy.py

USER """
USER Leave Policy Rules (BR-EL-001..015).
USER Pure Python implementation without Django/DRF/Celery imports.
USER """
USER 
USER from datetime import date, timedelta
USER from decimal import Decimal
USER from typing import Dict, Optional
USER 
USER 
USER class PolicyError(Exception):
USER     """Domain policy error."""
USER     pass
USER 
USER 
USER CATEGORIES = ("CL", "RH", "SCL", "EL", "COL", "VL")
USER 
USER ENTITLEMENT: Dict[str, tuple] = {
USER     "CL": (Decimal("8"), True, True, False),  # BR-EL-002
USER     "RH": (Decimal("2"), True, True, False),  # BR-EL-003
USER     "SCL": (Decimal("15"), True, True, False),  # BR-EL-004
USER     "EL": (Decimal("30"), False, True, True),  # BR-EL-005
USER     "COL": (Decimal("20"), True, True, True),  # BR-EL-009
USER     "VL": (Decimal("60"), True, False, False),  # BR-EL-006
USER }
USER 
USER CONTINUOUS = {"EL", "COL", "VL"}
USER 
USER 
USER def entitlement(category: str, kind: str) -> Optional[Decimal]:
USER     """
USER     Return the annual leave entitlement for a category and kind.
USER     
USER     - kind="faculty" returns faculty allowance (or None if not eligible).
USER     - Any other kind (e.g., "staff", "employee", "") is treated as staff.
USER     - Returns None if the category is not supported or the user kind is ineligible.
USER     
USER     BR-EL-001: Only supported categories allowed.
USER     BR-EL-005: EL is for non-faculty only (faculty_allowed=False).
USER     """
USER     if category not in CATEGORIES:
USER         raise PolicyError(f"Unknown leave category: {category}")  # BR-EL-001
USER     
USER     days_per_year, faculty_allowed, staff_allowed, carries_forward = ENTITLEMENT[category]
USER     
USER     is_faculty = kind == "faculty"
USER     
USER     if is_faculty:
USER         if faculty_allowed:
USER             return days_per_year
USER         else:
USER             return None  # EL is not for faculty
USER     else:
USER         # Any kind other than "faculty" is treated as staff
USER         if staff_allowed:
USER             return days_per_year
USER         else:
USER             return None  # VL is not for staff (faculty_allowed=True, staff_allowed=False)
USER 
USER 
USER def count_days(category: str, start: date, end: date, half_day: str = "none") -> Decimal:
USER     """
USER     Calculate the number of leave days consumed.
USER     
USER     BR-EL-010: Half-day CL consumes 0.5 day (only for CL and when start==end).
USER     BR-EL-011: EL, COL, VL count every calendar day inclusive (weekends/holidays included).
USER                CL, RH, SCL count Monday to Friday only (no holiday calendar yet -> assume working days).
USER     
USER     Note: Since no external calendar service is imported, we assume the input dates represent
USER     the requested interval. For CL/RH/SCL, we will perform a basic weekday check assuming
USER     standard work weeks (Mon-Fri) as per BR-EL-011 implication for non-continuous categories.
USER     """
USER     if start > end:
USER         raise PolicyError("End date must be on or after start date")  # Generic validation
USER     
USER     if half_day != "none" and category not in ("CL",):
USER         raise PolicyError(f"Half-day consumption only applies to CL. Category: {category}")  # BR-EL-010
USER     
USER     if half_day != "none" and start != end:
USER         raise PolicyError("Half-day consumption only allowed for single-day requests")  # BR-EL-010
USER     
USER     category_upper = category.upper()
USER     
USER     if category_upper in CONTINUOUS:
USER         # BR-EL-011: EL, COL, VL count every calendar day inclusive.
USER         # No filtering for weekends/holidays.
USER         days = Decimal(str((end - start).days + 1))
USER     else:
USER         # CL, RH, SCL: Count Monday to Friday only.
USER         # Since no holiday calendar is available, we assume standard working days (Mon-Fri).
USER         days = Decimal("0")
USER         current = start
USER         while current <= end:
USER             if current.weekday() < 5:  # Monday=0 to Friday=4
USER                 days += Decimal("1")
USER             current += timedelta(days=1)
USER     
USER     if half_day != "none":
USER         # Apply half-day consumption (0.5 day) directly per BR-EL-010
USER         return Decimal("0.5")
USER     
USER     return days
USER 
USER 
USER def overlaps(a_start: date, a_end: date, a_half: str, b_start: date, b_end: date, b_half: str) -> bool:
USER     """
USER     Check if two leave requests overlap.
USER     
USER     True when the date ranges intersect, except a single shared date where both halves are set and different.
USER     BR-EL-012: Applicant non-overlap with half-day CL exception.
USER     """
USER     # Determine if ranges intersect
USER     # Intersection exists if: start_a <= end_b AND start_b <= end_a
USER     intersects = (a_start <= b_end) and (b_start <= a_end)
USER     
USER     if not intersects:
USER         return False
USER     
USER     # Check for the half-day exception on the same date
USER     # Exception applies only to CL (implied by context of BR-EL-012 regarding halves)
USER     # If both are on the same day and have different halves, they do NOT overlap.
USER     if a_start == b_start:
USER         if a_half != "none" and b_half != "none":
USER             # Both are half-days on the same day
USER             if a_half != b_half:
USER                 return False  # Different halves on same date -> non-overlapping
USER     
USER     return True
USER 
USER 
USER def unit_head_is_final(category: str) -> bool:
USER     """
USER     Determine if Unit Head has final decision power.
USER     
USER     BR-EL-016: True for CL and RH.
USER     BR-EL-017: Others only get a recommendation.
USER     """
USER     return category in ("CL", "RH")
USER ```

USER ../elm_specs_md/pilot_kit/phase2/DESIGN.md
USER ```
USER # ELM Phase 2 target design (review decisions; code is written by the local model)
USER 
USER Rules: no user table (people are integer user ids from request.principal.user_id); never import another module's models; domain/ is pure Python; all writes in services/ inside transaction.atomic; every state change appends a LeaveAction row.
USER 
USER ## models.py
USER - LeaveCategory(TextChoices): CL, RH, SCL, EL, COL, VL. CharField max_length=3.
USER - HalfDay(TextChoices): NONE="none", FIRST="first", SECOND="second".
USER - Stage(TextChoices): SUBSTITUTE="substitute", UNIT_HEAD="unit_head", ESTABLISHMENT="establishment", SANCTION="sanction", DONE="done".
USER - Status(TextChoices): PENDING="pending", APPROVED="approved", REJECTED="rejected", WITHDRAWN="withdrawn", CANCELLED="cancelled".
USER - SubstituteStatus(TextChoices): NOT_REQUIRED="not_required", PENDING="pending", ACCEPTED="accepted", DECLINED="declined".
USER - RequestKind(TextChoices): LEAVE="leave", CANCELLATION="cancellation".
USER - ReportingLine(TimeStampedModel): user_id IntegerField(unique=True), unit_head_id IntegerField, establishment_id IntegerField(null=True, blank=True), sanctioning_authority_id IntegerField(null=True, blank=True).
USER - LeaveApplication(UserScopedModel, TimeStampedModel): kind, parent ForeignKey("self", null=True, blank=True, on_delete=PROTECT, related_name="cancellations"), category, start_date, end_date, half_day (default NONE), days DecimalField(max_digits=5, decimal_places=1), purpose TextField, station_leave BooleanField(default=False), station_address TextField(blank=True), substitute_id IntegerField(null=True, blank=True), substitute_status (default NOT_REQUIRED), unit_head_id IntegerField, establishment_id IntegerField(null=True, blank=True), sanctioning_authority_id IntegerField(null=True, blank=True), unit_head_recommendation CharField(max_length=20, blank=True), stage, status (default PENDING), assignee_id IntegerField(null=True, blank=True, db_index=True), decided_at DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True). Meta ordering ["-created_at"].
USER - LeaveBalance(UserScopedModel, TimeStampedModel): category, year IntegerField, entitled DecimalField(5,1), used DecimalField(5,1, default=0). UniqueConstraint(user_id, year, category). property available = entitled - used.
USER - LeaveAction(models.Model): application ForeignKey(LeaveApplication, CASCADE, related_name="actions"), actor_id IntegerField, action CharField(40), remark TextField(blank=True), created_at DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True). Meta ordering ["created_at"].
USER 
USER ## domain/policy.py (pure Python, Decimal arithmetic)
USER - ENTITLEMENT = {category: (days_per_year, faculty_allowed, staff_allowed, carries_forward)}: CL (8, yes, yes, no) BR-EL-002; RH (2, yes, yes, no) BR-EL-003; SCL (15, yes, yes, no) BR-EL-004; EL (30, no, yes, yes) BR-EL-005; COL (20, yes, yes, yes) BR-EL-009; VL (60, yes, no, no) BR-EL-006.
USER - entitlement(category, kind) -> Decimal | None. kind "faculty" means faculty, anything else is staff. None means not eligible (BR-EL-001).
USER - count_days(category, start, end, half_day) -> Decimal. Raise PolicyError if end < start. half_day other than "none" only for CL and only when start == end, giving 0.5 (BR-EL-010). EL, COL, VL count every calendar day inclusive (BR-EL-011). CL, RH, SCL count Monday to Friday only (no holiday calendar yet).
USER - overlaps(a_start, a_end, a_half, b_start, b_end, b_half) -> bool. True when the date ranges intersect, except a single shared date where both halves are set and different (BR-EL-012).
USER - unit_head_is_final(category) -> bool: True for CL and RH (BR-EL-016); others only get a recommendation (BR-EL-017).
USER - class PolicyError(Exception).
USER 
USER ## Workflow (services)
USER apply -> stage SUBSTITUTE (assignee = substitute) if a substitute is nominated, else UNIT_HEAD (assignee = unit head).
USER Substitute accepts -> UNIT_HEAD. Declines -> substitute_status DECLINED, assignee None; applicant nominates a replacement (back to SUBSTITUTE) or withdraws (BR-EL-015).
USER UNIT_HEAD: CL/RH approve or reject is final. Other categories: "recommended" or "not_recommended" (not a rejection), then ESTABLISHMENT if establishment_id else SANCTION.
USER ESTABLISHMENT: verify -> SANCTION (assignee = sanctioning authority).
USER SANCTION: approve or reject is final.
USER If the applicant is their own unit head (unit_head_id == user_id, e.g. Director), skip UNIT_HEAD and go to SANCTION; if there is no sanctioning authority either, the applicant self-sanctions (BR-EL-020).
USER Approval of a LEAVE request adds days to LeaveBalance.used for the start year. Rejection or withdrawal changes no balance.
USER Withdraw: owner only, only while PENDING (BR-EL-022). Pending requests are never edited (BR-EL-034).
USER Cancellation: owner asks before start_date for an APPROVED leave; creates a CANCELLATION request (parent = the leave) routed like the original (BR-EL-023). Approving it marks the parent CANCELLED and subtracts its days from used.
USER Only the current assignee may act; anyone else gets NotFound (shown as 404, so ids are not leaked).
USER 
USER ## Permissions (registry.py, code "leave")
USER leave.application.apply, leave.application.view_own, leave.substitute.respond, leave.application.review, leave.application.verify, leave.application.sanction, leave.admin.manage.
USER Roles: Employee = apply, view_own, substitute.respond. Unit Head = review. Deputy Registrar (Estt.) = review, verify. Dean, Registrar, Director = review, sanction. Leave Administrator = admin.manage.
USER Nav: /leave/apply (apply), /leave/mine (view_own), /leave/substitute (substitute.respond), /leave/approvals (review).
USER 
USER ## API (base /api/v1/leave/)
USER GET/POST applications/ ; GET applications/<id>/ ; POST applications/<id>/withdraw/ ; POST applications/<id>/cancel/ ;
USER POST applications/<id>/substitute-response/ {"accept": bool} ; POST applications/<id>/replace-substitute/ {"substitute_id": int} ;
USER GET inbox/ ; POST applications/<id>/decide/ {"decision": str} ; GET balances/?year= ; GET/PUT reporting-lines/<user_id>/ (admin).
USER Errors reuse Fusion's core.api.exceptions: services raise LeaveError (class LeaveError(BadRequestError), HTTP 400) and NotFound (class NotFound(NotFoundError), HTTP 404); selectors raise core.api.exceptions.NotFoundError directly. The platform exception handler (core.api.exceptions.exception_handler) renders the standard {"error": {"code", "message", "details", "request_id"}} envelope, so views never catch these or build error responses themselves.

USER from django.db import transaction
USER 
USER from core.api.exceptions import BadRequestError, NotFoundError
USER from modules.leave.domain import policy
USER from modules.leave.models import (
USER     LeaveAction, LeaveApplication, LeaveBalance, ReportingLine,
USER     RequestKind, Stage, Status, SubstituteStatus,
USER )
USER 
USER 
USER class LeaveError(BadRequestError):
USER     code = "leave_error"
USER 
USER 
USER class NotFound(NotFoundError):
USER     pass
USER 
USER 
USER def log_action(app: LeaveApplication, actor_id: int, action: str, remark: str = "") -> None:
USER     """Append an entry to the leave action history."""
USER     LeaveAction.objects.create(
USER         application=app,
USER         actor_id=actor_id,
USER         action=action,
USER         remark=remark,
USER     )
USER 
USER 
USER def get_balance(user_id: int, kind: str, category: str, year: int) -> LeaveBalance:
USER     """Get or create leave balance for a user.
USER 
USER     Raises LeaveError if the user is not eligible for the category/kind combination.
USER     """
USER     try:
USER         entitlement = policy.entitlement(category, kind)
USER     except policy.PolicyError as e:
USER         raise LeaveError(str(e))
USER 
USER     if entitlement is None:
USER         raise LeaveError(f"Not eligible for {category} leave as {kind}.")
USER 
USER     balance, created = LeaveBalance.objects.get_or_create(
USER         user_id=user_id,
USER         year=year,
USER         category=category,
USER         defaults={
USER             "entitled": policy.ENTITLEMENT[category][0],
USER             "used": Decimal("0"),
USER         },
USER     )
USER 
USER     if not created:
USER         # Ensure entitled matches policy (in case of manual edits or data sync issues)
USER         balance.entitled = policy.ENTITLEMENT[category][0]
USER         balance.save()
USER 
USER     return balance
USER 
USER 
USER def has_overlap(user_id: int, start: date, end: date, half_day: str, exclude_id: Optional[int] = None) -> bool:
USER     """Check if the user has an overlapping leave application.
USER 
USER     Considers PENDING or APPROVED LEAVE-kind applications.
USER     Applies BR-EL-012 exception for CL half-days on single dates.
USER     """
USER     from datetime import date as DateType
USER     
USER     # Determine which categories count as "LEAVE" for overlap purposes (excluding CANCELLATION)
USER     leave_categories = [cat[0] for cat in LeaveApplication._meta.get_field('category').choices if cat[0] != 'CANCELLATION']
USER 
USER     applications = LeaveApplication.objects.filter(
USER         user_id=user_id,
USER         category__in=leave_categories,
USER         status__in=[Status.PENDING.value, Status.APPROVED.value],
USER     )
USER 
USER     # Exclude the current application if provided
USER     if exclude_id is not None:
USER         applications = applications.exclude(id=exclude_id)
USER 
USER     for app in applications:
USER         # Calculate overlap using policy logic
USER         try:
USER             is_overlapping = policy.overlaps(app.start_date, app.end_date, app.half_day, start, end, half_day)
USER             if is_overlapping:
USER                 return True
USER         except Exception:
USER             continue
USER 
USER     return False
USER 
USER 
USER def route_to_approver(app: LeaveApplication) -> None:
USER     """Route the application to the appropriate approver based on hierarchy.
USER 
USER     - If unit_head_id == user_id (self-sanctioning authority): go to SANCTION.
USER     - If no sanctioning_authority_id: self-sanction (SANCTION).
USER     - Otherwise: route to UNIT_HEAD first, then ESTABLISHMENT, then SANCTION.
USER     """
USER     # Determine the initial assignee based on category and hierarchy
USER     if app.unit_head_id == app.user_id:
USER         # Self-sanctioning authority (e.g., Director)
USER         app.stage = Stage.SANCTION.value
USER         app.assignee_id = app.sanctioning_authority_id or app.user_id
USER     else:
USER         # Standard hierarchy: Unit Head -> Establishment -> Sanctioning Authority
USER         # Per DESIGN.md, we route to UNIT_HEAD first for review/recommendation
USER         app.stage = Stage.UNIT_HEAD.value
USER         app.assignee_id = app.unit_head_id
USER 
USER     app.save()
USER 
USER 
USER def apply_leave(user_id: int, kind: str, data: dict) -> LeaveApplication:
USER     """Create and submit a new leave application.
USER 
USER     Validates eligibility, balance, overlaps, and substitutes.
USER     Routes the application to the appropriate approver.
USER     """
USER     from datetime import date as DateType
USER     
USER     category = data["category"]
USER     start_date = data["start_date"]
USER     end_date = data["end_date"]
USER     half_day = data.get("half_day", "none")
USER     purpose = data["purpose"]
USER     station_leave = data.get("station_leave", False)
USER     station_address = data.get("station_address", "")
USER     substitute_id = data.get("substitute_id")
USER 
USER     # 1. Check eligibility (BR-EL-001, BR-EL-005, etc.)
USER     try:
USER         entitlement = policy.entitlement(category, kind)
USER     except policy.PolicyError as e:
USER         raise LeaveError(str(e))
USER 
USER     if entitlement is None:
USER         raise LeaveError(f"Not eligible for {category} leave as {kind}.")
USER 
USER     # 2. Compute days (BR-EL-010, BR-EL-011)
USER     try:
USER         days = policy.count_days(category, start_date, end_date, half_day)
USER     except policy.PolicyError as e:
USER         raise LeaveError(str(e))
USER 
USER     if days <= 0:
USER         raise LeaveError(f"Invalid leave duration: {days} days.")
USER 
USER     # 3. Check balance (BR-EL-025)
USER     try:
USER         balance = get_balance(user_id, kind, category, start_date.year)
USER     except LeaveError as e:
USER         raise LeaveError(str(e))
USER 
USER     if balance.available < days:
USER         raise LeaveError(f"Insufficient leave balance. Available: {balance.available}, Required: {days}.")
USER 
USER     # 4. Check overlap (BR-EL-012)
USER     if has_overlap(user_id, start_date, end_date, half_day):
USER         raise LeaveError("Leave application overlaps with existing pending or approved leave.")
USER 
USER     # 5. Handle substitute logic (BR-EL-014)
USER     if substitute_id is not None:
USER         # Reject if substitute is the applicant
USER         if substitute_id == user_id:
USER             raise LeaveError("Substitute cannot be the applicant.")
USER 
USER         # Check if substitute has overlapping leave of ANY category
USER         sub_apps = LeaveApplication.objects.filter(
USER             user_id=substitute_id,
USER             status__in=[Status.PENDING.value, Status.APPROVED.value],
USER         )
USER 
USER         for sub_app in sub_apps:
USER             try:
USER                 is_overlapping = policy.overlaps(sub_app.start_date, sub_app.end_date, sub_app.half_day, start_date, end_date, half_day)
USER                 if is_overlapping:
USER                     raise LeaveError(f"Substitute {substitute_id} has an overlapping leave application.")
USER             except Exception:
USER                 continue
USER 
USER         # Set stage to SUBSTITUTE
USER         app, created = LeaveApplication.objects.create(
USER             user_id=user_id,
USER             kind=RequestKind.LEAVE.value,
USER             category=category,
USER             start_date=start_date,
USER             end_date=end_date,
USER             half_day=half_day,
USER             purpose=purpose,
USER             station_leave=station_leave,
USER             station_address=station_address,
USER             substitute_id=substitute_id,
USER             unit_head_id=ReportingLine.objects.get(user_id=user_id).unit_head_id,
USER             establishment_id=ReportingLine.objects.get(user_id=user_id).establishment_id,
USER             sanctioning_authority_id=ReportingLine.objects.get(user_id=user_id).sanctioning_authority_id,
USER         )
USER 
USER         app.stage = Stage.SUBSTITUTE.value
USER         app.assignee_id = substitute_id
USER         app.substitute_status = SubstituteStatus.PENDING.value
USER     else:
USER         # No substitute nominated
USER         app, created = LeaveApplication.objects.create(
USER             user_id=user_id,
USER             kind=RequestKind.LEAVE.value,
USER             category=category,
USER             start_date=start_date,
USER             end_date=end_date,
USER             half_day=half_day,
USER             purpose=purpose,
USER             station_leave=station_leave,
USER             station_address=station_address,
USER             substitute_id=None,
USER             unit_head_id=ReportingLine.objects.get(user_id=user_id).unit_head_id,
USER             establishment_id=ReportingLine.objects.get(user_id=user_id).establishment_id,
USER             sanctioning_authority_id=ReportingLine.objects.get(user_id=user_id).sanctioning_authority_id,
USER         )
USER 
USER     # 6. Load ReportingLine (BR-EL-018) - Already done above via create defaults
USER     
USER     # 7. Route to approver (BR-EL-015, BR-EL-020)
USER     route_to_approver(app)
USER 
USER     # Log action
USER     log_action(app, user_id, "apply", f"Leave application submitted for {category} leave.")
USER 
USER     return app
USER 
USER 
USER def respond_to_substitute(actor_id: int, application_id: int, accept: bool) -> LeaveApplication:
USER     """Accept or decline a substitute request.
USER 
USER     Only the current assignee (substitute) may respond.
USER     """
USER     app = LeaveApplication.objects.select_for_update().get(id=application_id)
USER 
USER     if app.assignee_id != actor_id:
USER         raise NotFound(f"Only the assigned substitute can respond to this application.")
USER 
USER     if app.status not in [Status.PENDING.value, Stage.SUBSTITUTE.value]:  # Assuming 'pending' or 'submitted' states allow response
USER         raise NotFound(f"Cannot respond to substitute request for application in status {app.status}.")
USER 
USER     old_status = app.status
USER     if accept:
USER         new_status = Status.PENDING.value  # Keep pending but mark substitute accepted
USER         description = "Substitute consent accepted."
USER         app.substitute_status = SubstituteStatus.ACCEPTED.value
USER         # Route to approver after acceptance
USER         route_to_approver(app)
USER     else:
USER         new_status = Status.PENDING.value  # Revert to applicant action required state
USER         description = "Substitute consent declined."
USER         app.substitute_status = SubstituteStatus.DECLINED.value
USER         app.assignee_id = None
USER 
USER     app.save()
USER 
USER     log_action(app, actor_id, "substitute_response", description)
USER 
USER     return app
USER 
USER 
USER def replace_substitute(actor_id: int, application_id: int, substitute_id: int) -> LeaveApplication:
USER     """Nominate a replacement substitute after a decline.
USER 
USER     Only the owner (applicant) may replace a declined substitute.
USER     """
USER     app = LeaveApplication.objects.select_for_update().get(id=application_id)
USER 
USER     if app.user_id != actor_id:
USER         raise NotFound(f"Only the application owner can nominate a replacement substitute.")
USER 
USER     if app.status not in [Status.PENDING.value]:
USER         raise NotFound(f"Can only replace a declined substitute. Current status: {app.status}.")
USER 
USER     if app.substitute_status != SubstituteStatus.DECLINED.value:
USER         raise NotFound(f"Can only replace a declined substitute. Current status: {app.substitute_status}.")
USER 
USER     # Validate new substitute eligibility (simplified: not applicant, no overlap)
USER     if substitute_id == actor_id:
USER         raise LeaveError("Substitute cannot be the applicant.")
USER 
USER     # Check for existing pending/approved leave of the new substitute that overlaps
USER     sub_apps = LeaveApplication.objects.filter(
USER         user_id=substitute_id,
USER         status__in=[Status.PENDING.value, Status.APPROVED.value],
USER     )
USER 
USER     for sub_app in sub_apps:
USER         try:
USER             is_overlapping = policy.overlaps(sub_app.start_date, sub_app.end_date, sub_app.half_day, app.start_date, app.end_date, app.half_day)
USER             if is_overlapping:
USER                 raise LeaveError(f"New substitute {substitute_id} has an overlapping leave application.")
USER         except Exception:
USER             continue
USER 
USER     # Update the nominated substitute ID and status
USER     app.substitute_id = substitute_id
USER     app.substitute_status = SubstituteStatus.PENDING.value
USER     app.stage = Stage.SUBSTITUTE.value
USER     app.assignee_id = substitute_id
USER     app.save()
USER 
USER     log_action(app, actor_id, "replace_substitute", f"New substitute {substitute_id} nominated.")
USER 
USER     return app
USER 
USER 
USER def withdraw(actor_id: int, application_id: int) -> LeaveApplication:
USER     """Withdraw a pending leave request.
USER 
USER     Only the owner may withdraw a PENDING request.
USER     """
USER     app = LeaveApplication.objects.select_for_update().get(id=application_id)
USER 
USER     if app.user_id != actor_id:
USER         raise NotFound(f"Only the application owner can withdraw this application.")
USER 
USER     if app.status not in [Status.PENDING.value]:
USER         raise NotFound(f"Cannot withdraw application in status {app.status}.")
USER 
USER     old_status = app.status
USER     app.status = Status.WITHDRAWN.value
USER     app.assignee_id = None
USER     app.save()
USER 
USER     log_action(app, actor_id, "withdraw", f"Application withdrawn by user {actor_id}.")
USER 
USER     return app

USER @transaction.atomic
USER def withdraw_pending_request(
USER     actor_id: int,
USER     application_id: int,
USER ) -> None:
USER     """
USER     Withdraw a pending leave request before final approval.
USER     
USER     Preconditions:
USER     - The application status must be 'draft' or 'submitted' (but not yet approved/rejected/etc).
USER     - Final approval has not occurred.
USER     
USER     Postconditions:
USER     - Application status is set to 'withdrawn'.
USER     - History entry is appended.
USER     
USER     Raises:
USER         ValueError: If the application is already approved, rejected, or in a state where withdrawal is not allowed.
USER     """
USER     application = LeaveApplication.objects.get(id=application_id)
USER 
USER     # Check if withdrawal is allowed (must be pending and not finally approved/rejected)
USER     allowed_statuses = ['draft', 'submitted', 'under_review', 'recommended', 'not_recommended']
USER     
USER     # Exception: If it was recommended/not_recommended by Unit Head, we can still withdraw before final sanction?
USER     # Per EL-UC-007: "Immediately terminate a pending request before final approval."
USER     # "Final approval" implies the decision from the highest competent authority.
USER     # For CL/RH, Unit Head is final. For SCL/EL/COL/VL, Final Sanctioning Authority is final.
USER     # So if status is 'approved' (by Unit Head for CL/RH) or 'recommended' (by Unit Head for SCL/EL...) 
USER     # we need to check who made the decision.
USER     
USER     category_enum = ModelLeaveCategory(application.category)
USER     
USER     # Determine if this is a "final" decision point
USER     # If Unit Head made a final decision (approve/reject), withdrawal is NOT allowed.
USER     # If Unit Head made a recommendation, and it's not yet routed to Final Sanctioning Authority, 
USER     # withdrawal might be allowed? The spec says "before final approval".
USER     # Let's interpret "final approval" as the state where the leave is officially approved/rejected by the highest authority.
USER     # However, EL-UC-007 mentions "prevents later approve/reject actions".
USER     # If status is 'approved' or 'rejected', withdrawal is definitely not allowed.
USER     if application.status in ['approved', 'rejected']:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Cannot withdraw an {application.status} application.")
USER 
USER     # Check for overlap with other active leaves? 
USER     # Usually withdrawal removes the pending leave, so overlap check might be relaxed or handled differently.
USER     # But let's assume we just need to ensure no final decision exists.
USER     
USER     if application.status not in allowed_statuses:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Cannot withdraw application in status {application.status}.")
USER 
USER     # Update status to withdrawn
USER     old_status = application.status
USER     application.status = 'withdrawn'
USER     application.save()
USER 
USER     _log_action_history(

USER         action_type="withdrawal",

USER             'category': category_enum,
USER             'requested_from': application.requested_from,
USER             'requested_to': application.requested_to,
USER             'purpose': f"Resumption of duty for leave {application.id}",
USER             'station_leave_details': None,
USER             'nominated_substitute_id': None,
USER             'status': 'submitted',  # Placeholder for review state
USER         },
USER     )
USER 
USER     if not created:
USER         resumption_app.status = 'submitted'
USER         resumption_app.save()
USER 
USER     # Log history for original application
USER     _log_action_history(
USER         application=application,
USER         actor_id=actor_id,
USER         action_type="resumption_submitted",
USER         description=f"Resumption submitted on {actual_resumption_date}.",
USER     )
USER 
USER     return resumption_app

USER @transaction.atomic
USER def review_resumption(
USER     actor_id: int,
USER     application_id: int,
USER     actual_resumption_date: date,
USER ) -> None:
USER     """
USER     Review the submitted resumption and adjust balance for early or late return.
USER     
USER     Preconditions:
USER     - A valid resumption submission exists (status 'submitted' or similar).
USER     - Actor is the applicable Sanctioning Authority.
USER     
USER     Postconditions:
USER     - If actual date matches approved end date: No adjustment.
USER     - If actual date is earlier/late: Adjust balance and record actual leave period.
USER     - History entry appended.
USER     
USER     Raises:
USER         ValueError: If application status not allowed or date invalid.
USER     """
USER     resumption_app = LeaveApplication.objects.get(id=application_id)
USER 
USER     category_enum = ModelLeaveCategory(resumption_app.category)
USER     
USER     # Check if category allows resumption review (EL, COL, VL)
USER     if category_enum not in [ModelLeaveCategory.EL, ModelLeaveCategory.COL, ModelLeaveCategory.VL]:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Resumption review is only applicable for EL, COL, or VL categories.")
USER 
USER     allowed_statuses = ['submitted']  # Assuming 'submitted' represents the resumption review state
USER     if resumption_app.status not in allowed_statuses:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Cannot review resumption for application in status {resumption_app.status}.")
USER 
USER     # Compare actual date with approved end date (requested_to)
USER     approved_end_date = resumption_app.requested_to
USER     
USER     if actual_resumption_date == approved_end_date:
USER         description = f"Resumption date matches approved end date ({approved_end_date}). No adjustment required."
USER     elif actual_resumption_date < approved_end_date:
USER         # Early return: Leave ended before approved. 
USER         # Balance adjustment: The employee used fewer days than approved? Or the approved period was extended?
USER         # Usually, if you return early, the leave period is shortened. 
USER         # We need to adjust the balance for the unused portion? Or the consumed portion?
USER         # BR-EL-027/028: "adjusts balance accordingly".
USER         # If returned early, the employee has not used the full approved period. 
USER         # So we should add back the unused days to available balance?
USER         # But wait, the leave was already taken (used_days increased). 
USER         # If they return early, maybe the 'used_days' is correct for the actual period, and we need to adjust the 'available_days' for the remaining approved period?
USER         # Let's assume: 
USER         # Approved period: [start, end]
USER         # Actual period: [start, actual_end]
USER         # If actual_end < end, then the employee used (actual_end - start) days.
USER         # The approved leave was (end - start + 1) days.
USER         # So unused days = (end - start + 1) - (actual_end - start + 1) = end - actual_end.
USER         # We should add these unused days back to available balance? 
USER         # But the employee already used them. 
USER         # Actually, if they return early, it means they didn't take the full leave. 
USER         # So the 'used_days' in LeaveBalance should reflect the actual usage. 
USER         # If the system recorded used_days based on approved period, we need to correct it.
USER         # Let's assume we adjust the balance by adding back the unused days (end - actual_end).
USER         consumed_days = calculate_consumed_days(approved_end_date, resumption_app.requested_from)
USER         actual_consumed_days = calculate_consumed_days(actual_resumption_date, resumption_app.requested_from)
USER         unused_days = consumed_days - actual_consumed_days
USER         
USER         if unused_days > 0:
USER             _update_balance_on_resumption(resumption_app, actor_id, 'add', unused_days)
USER             description = f"Early return on {actual_resumption_date}. Added {unused_days} days to balance."
USER         else:
USER             description = f"Early return on {actual_resumption_date}. No adjustment (negative unused)."
USER     else:  # actual_resumption_date > approved_end_date
USER         # Late return: Employee took more time than approved.
USER         # This might be an extension or just late return.
USER         # If it's just late return, the leave period was extended de facto.
USER         # We need to adjust balance for the extra days used?
USER         # Or maybe the original approval was wrong?
USER         # Let's assume we add the extra days to used_days (if not already done) and adjust available balance accordingly?
USER         # Actually, if they return late, it means they used more days than approved.
USER         # So we should increase used_days and decrease available_days? 
USER         # But the leave was already taken. 
USER         # Let's assume we calculate the difference and adjust.
USER         consumed_days = calculate_consumed_days(approved_end_date, resumption_app.requested_from)
USER         actual_consumed_days = calculate_consumed_days(actual_resumption_date, resumption_app.requested_from)
USER         extra_days = actual_consumed_days - consumed_days
USER         
USER         if extra_days > 0:
USER             _update_balance_on_resumption(resumption_app, actor_id, 'subtract', extra_days)
USER             description = f"Late return on {actual_resumption_date}. Subtracted {extra_days} days from balance."
USER         else:
USER             description = f"Late return on {actual_resumption_date}. No adjustment (negative extra)."
USER 
USER     # Update the resumption application status to indicate review completion
USER     resumption_app.status = 'approved'  # Or some completed state
USER     resumption_app.save()
USER 
USER     _log_action_history(
USER         application=resumption_app,
USER         actor_id=actor_id,
USER         action_type="resumption_reviewed",
USER         description=description,
USER     )
USER

USER def _restore_balance_on_cancellation(cancellation_app: LeaveApplication, actor_id: int) -> None:
USER     """
USER     Restore leave balance when cancellation is approved (BR-EL-023).
USER     
USER     This assumes the original leave was approved and used_days were increased.
USER     On cancellation approval, we need to revert the used_days or adjust available_days.
USER     Since we don't have a direct link between cancellation_app and original_app's balance changes,
USER     we'll assume the cancellation_app represents the reversal.
USER     
USER     We'll update the balance for the category/year of the original leave (which should be same as cancellation_app).
USER     """
USER     from modules.leave.models import LeaveBalance
USER     
USER     # Determine year (simplified: use requested_from year)
USER     year = cancellation_app.requested_from.year
USER     
USER     # Calculate consumed days for the original period (before cancellation)
USER     # We don't have the original application here, but we can assume the cancellation_app 
USER     # has the same category and dates as the original.
USER     # Let's assume we need to restore the balance that was deducted.
USER     # If the original leave was approved, used_days were increased.
USER     # On cancellation approval, we should decrease used_days or increase available_days.
USER     # Let's increase available_days by the number of days in the original period (assuming full usage was recorded).
USER     # Or better: calculate the difference between approved and actual usage? 
USER     # But for cancellation, it's a reversal of the entire approved leave.
USER     
USER     # We'll assume the balance record exists or create it.
USER     # Let's add back the days that were used (assuming full usage was recorded).
USER     # Or simply set available_days to the original entitlement?
USER     # To be safe, let's calculate consumed days for the period and add them back.
USER     
USER     from datetime import timedelta
USER     
USER     start_date = cancellation_app.requested_from
USER     end_date = cancellation_app.requested_to
USER     
USER     total_days = (end_date - start_date).days + 1
USER     
USER     # Query or create balance record
USER     try:
USER         balance = LeaveBalance.objects.get(
USER             user_id=actor_id,
USER             year=year,
USER             category=cancellation_app.category
USER         )
USER     except LeaveBalance.DoesNotExist:
USER         balance = LeaveBalance.objects.create(
USER             user_id=actor_id,
USER             year=year,
USER             category=cancellation_app.category,
USER             available_days=0.0,
USER             used_days=0.0
USER         )
USER     
USER     # Restore the balance (add back the used days)
USER     # Note: This is a simplification. In reality, we might need to know how many days were actually used before cancellation.
USER     # Assuming full usage was recorded for an approved leave.
USER     balance.available_days += float(total_days)
USER     balance.save()
USER

USER modules/leave/domain/policy.py
USER ```
USER """
USER Leave Policy Rules (BR-EL-001..015).
USER Pure Python implementation without Django/DRF/Celery imports.
USER """
USER 
USER from datetime import date, timedelta
USER from decimal import Decimal
USER from typing import Dict, Optional
USER 
USER 
USER class PolicyError(Exception):
USER     """Domain policy error."""
USER     pass
USER 
USER 
USER CATEGORIES = ("CL", "RH", "SCL", "EL", "COL", "VL")
USER 
USER ENTITLEMENT: Dict[str, tuple] = {
USER     "CL": (Decimal("8"), True, True, False),  # BR-EL-002
USER     "RH": (Decimal("2"), True, True, False),  # BR-EL-003
USER     "SCL": (Decimal("15"), True, True, False),  # BR-EL-004
USER     "EL": (Decimal("30"), False, True, True),  # BR-EL-005
USER     "COL": (Decimal("20"), True, True, True),  # BR-EL-009
USER     "VL": (Decimal("60"), True, False, False),  # BR-EL-006
USER }
USER 
USER CONTINUOUS = {"EL", "COL", "VL"}
USER 
USER 
USER def entitlement(category: str, kind: str) -> Optional[Decimal]:
USER     """
USER     Return the annual leave entitlement for a category and kind.
USER     
USER     - kind="faculty" returns faculty allowance (or None if not eligible).
USER     - Any other kind (e.g., "staff", "employee", "") is treated as staff.
USER     - Returns None if the category is not supported or the user kind is ineligible.
USER     
USER     BR-EL-001: Only supported categories allowed.
USER     BR-EL-005: EL is for non-faculty only (faculty_allowed=False).
USER     """
USER     if category not in CATEGORIES:
USER         raise PolicyError(f"Unknown leave category: {category}")  # BR-EL-001
USER     
USER     days_per_year, faculty_allowed, staff_allowed, carries_forward = ENTITLEMENT[category]
USER     
USER     is_faculty = kind == "faculty"
USER     
USER     if is_faculty:
USER         if faculty_allowed:
USER             return days_per_year
USER         else:
USER             return None  # EL is not for faculty
USER     else:
USER         # Any kind other than "faculty" is treated as staff
USER         if staff_allowed:
USER             return days_per_year
USER         else:
USER             return None  # VL is not for staff (faculty_allowed=True, staff_allowed=False)
USER 
USER 
USER def count_days(category: str, start: date, end: date, half_day: str = "none") -> Decimal:
USER     """
USER     Calculate the number of leave days consumed.
USER     
USER     BR-EL-010: Half-day CL consumes 0.5 day (only for CL and when start==end).
USER     BR-EL-011: EL, COL, VL count every calendar day inclusive (weekends/holidays included).
USER                CL, RH, SCL count Monday to Friday only (no holiday calendar yet -> assume working days).
USER     
USER     Note: Since no external calendar service is imported, we assume the input dates represent
USER     the requested interval. For CL/RH/SCL, we will perform a basic weekday check assuming
USER     standard work weeks (Mon-Fri) as per BR-EL-011 implication for non-continuous categories.
USER     """
USER     if start > end:
USER         raise PolicyError("End date must be on or after start date")  # Generic validation
USER     
USER     if half_day != "none" and category not in ("CL",):
USER         raise PolicyError(f"Half-day consumption only applies to CL. Category: {category}")  # BR-EL-010
USER     
USER     if half_day != "none" and start != end:
USER         raise PolicyError("Half-day consumption only allowed for single-day requests")  # BR-EL-010
USER     
USER     category_upper = category.upper()
USER     
USER     if category_upper in CONTINUOUS:
USER         # BR-EL-011: EL, COL, VL count every calendar day inclusive.
USER         # No filtering for weekends/holidays.
USER         days = Decimal(str((end - start).days + 1))
USER     else:
USER         # CL, RH, SCL: Count Monday to Friday only.
USER         # Since no holiday calendar is available, we assume standard working days (Mon-Fri).
USER         days = Decimal("0")
USER         current = start
USER         while current <= end:
USER             if current.weekday() < 5:  # Monday=0 to Friday=4
USER                 days += Decimal("1")
USER             current += timedelta(days=1)
USER     
USER     if half_day != "none":
USER         # Apply half-day consumption (0.5 day) directly per BR-EL-010
USER         return Decimal("0.5")
USER     
USER     return days
USER 
USER 
USER def overlaps(a_start: date, a_end: date, a_half: str, b_start: date, b_end: date, b_half: str) -> bool:
USER     """
USER     Check if two leave requests overlap.
USER     
USER     True when the date ranges intersect, except a single shared date where both halves are set and different.
USER     BR-EL-012: Applicant non-overlap with half-day CL exception.
USER     """
USER     # Determine if ranges intersect
USER     # Intersection exists if: start_a <= end_b AND start_b <= end_a
USER     intersects = (a_start <= b_end) and (b_start <= a_end)
USER     
USER     if not intersects:
USER         return False
USER     
USER     # Check for the half-day exception on the same date
USER     # Exception applies only to CL (implied by context of BR-EL-012 regarding halves)
USER     # If both are on the same day and have different halves, they do NOT overlap.
USER     if a_start == b_start:
USER         if a_half != "none" and b_half != "none":
USER             # Both are half-days on the same day
USER             if a_half != b_half:
USER                 return False  # Different halves on same date -> non-overlapping
USER     
USER     return True
USER 
USER 
USER def unit_head_is_final(category: str) -> bool:
USER     """
USER     Determine if Unit Head has final decision power.
USER     
USER     BR-EL-016: True for CL and RH.
USER     BR-EL-017: Others only get a recommendation.
USER     """
USER     return category in ("CL", "RH")
USER ```
