USER from django.db import transaction
USER 
USER from core.api.exceptions import BadRequestError, NotFoundError
USER from modules.leave.domain import policy
USER from modules.leave.models import (
USER     LeaveAction, LeaveApplication, LeaveBalance, ReportingLine,
USER     RequestKind, Stage, Status, SubstituteStatus,
USER )
USER 
USER 
USER class LeaveError(BadRequestError):
USER     code = "leave_error"
USER 
USER 
USER class NotFound(NotFoundError):
USER     pass
USER 
USER 
USER def log_action(app: LeaveApplication, actor_id: int, action: str, remark: str = "") -> None:
USER     """Append an entry to the leave action history."""
USER     LeaveAction.objects.create(
USER         application=app,
USER         actor_id=actor_id,
USER         action=action,
USER         remark=remark,
USER     )
USER 
USER 
USER def get_balance(user_id: int, kind: str, category: str, year: int) -> LeaveBalance:
USER     """Get or create leave balance for a user.
USER 
USER     Raises LeaveError if the user is not eligible for the category/kind combination.
USER     """
USER     try:
USER         entitlement = policy.entitlement(category, kind)
USER     except policy.PolicyError as e:
USER         raise LeaveError(str(e))
USER 
USER     if entitlement is None:
USER         raise LeaveError(f"Not eligible for {category} leave as {kind}.")
USER 
USER     balance, created = LeaveBalance.objects.get_or_create(
USER         user_id=user_id,
USER         year=year,
USER         category=category,
USER         defaults={
USER             "entitled": policy.ENTITLEMENT[category][0],
USER             "used": Decimal("0"),
USER         },
USER     )
USER 
USER     if not created:
USER         # Ensure entitled matches policy (in case of manual edits or data sync issues)
USER         balance.entitled = policy.ENTITLEMENT[category][0]
USER         balance.save()
USER 
USER     return balance
USER 
USER 
USER def has_overlap(user_id: int, start: date, end: date, half_day: str, exclude_id: Optional[int] = None) -> bool:
USER     """Check if the user has an overlapping leave application.
USER 
USER     Considers PENDING or APPROVED LEAVE-kind applications.
USER     Applies BR-EL-012 exception for CL half-days on single dates.
USER     """
USER     from datetime import date as DateType
USER     
USER     # Determine which categories count as "LEAVE" for overlap purposes (excluding CANCELLATION)
USER     leave_categories = [cat[0] for cat in LeaveApplication._meta.get_field('category').choices if cat[0] != 'CANCELLATION']
USER 
USER     applications = LeaveApplication.objects.filter(
USER         user_id=user_id,
USER         category__in=leave_categories,
USER         status__in=[Status.PENDING.value, Status.APPROVED.value],
USER     )
USER 
USER     # Exclude the current application if provided
USER     if exclude_id is not None:
USER         applications = applications.exclude(id=exclude_id)
USER 
USER     for app in applications:
USER         # Calculate overlap using policy logic
USER         try:
USER             is_overlapping = policy.overlaps(app.start_date, app.end_date, app.half_day, start, end, half_day)
USER             if is_overlapping:
USER                 return True
USER         except Exception:
USER             continue
USER 
USER     return False
USER 
USER 
USER def route_to_approver(app: LeaveApplication) -> None:
USER     """Route the application to the appropriate approver based on hierarchy.
USER 
USER     - If unit_head_id == user_id (self-sanctioning authority): go to SANCTION.
USER     - If no sanctioning_authority_id: self-sanction (SANCTION).
USER     - Otherwise: route to UNIT_HEAD first, then ESTABLISHMENT, then SANCTION.
USER     """
USER     # Determine the initial assignee based on category and hierarchy
USER     if app.unit_head_id == app.user_id:
USER         # Self-sanctioning authority (e.g., Director)
USER         app.stage = Stage.SANCTION.value
USER         app.assignee_id = app.sanctioning_authority_id or app.user_id
USER     else:
USER         # Standard hierarchy: Unit Head -> Establishment -> Sanctioning Authority
USER         # Per DESIGN.md, we route to UNIT_HEAD first for review/recommendation
USER         app.stage = Stage.UNIT_HEAD.value
USER         app.assignee_id = app.unit_head_id
USER 
USER     app.save()
USER 
USER 
USER def apply_leave(user_id: int, kind: str, data: dict) -> LeaveApplication:
USER     """Create and submit a new leave application.
USER 
USER     Validates eligibility, balance, overlaps, and substitutes.
USER     Routes the application to the appropriate approver.
USER     """
USER     from datetime import date as DateType
USER     
USER     category = data["category"]
USER     start_date = data["start_date"]
USER     end_date = data["end_date"]
USER     half_day = data.get("half_day", "none")
USER     purpose = data["purpose"]
USER     station_leave = data.get("station_leave", False)
USER     station_address = data.get("station_address", "")
USER     substitute_id = data.get("substitute_id")
USER 
USER     # 1. Check eligibility (BR-EL-001, BR-EL-005, etc.)
USER     try:
USER         entitlement = policy.entitlement(category, kind)
USER     except policy.PolicyError as e:
USER         raise LeaveError(str(e))
USER 
USER     if entitlement is None:
USER         raise LeaveError(f"Not eligible for {category} leave as {kind}.")
USER 
USER     # 2. Compute days (BR-EL-010, BR-EL-011)
USER     try:
USER         days = policy.count_days(category, start_date, end_date, half_day)
USER     except policy.PolicyError as e:
USER         raise LeaveError(str(e))
USER 
USER     if days <= 0:
USER         raise LeaveError(f"Invalid leave duration: {days} days.")
USER 
USER     # 3. Check balance (BR-EL-025)
USER     try:
USER         balance = get_balance(user_id, kind, category, start_date.year)
USER     except LeaveError as e:
USER         raise LeaveError(str(e))
USER 
USER     if balance.available < days:
USER         raise LeaveError(f"Insufficient leave balance. Available: {balance.available}, Required: {days}.")
USER 
USER     # 4. Check overlap (BR-EL-012)
USER     if has_overlap(user_id, start_date, end_date, half_day):
USER         raise LeaveError("Leave application overlaps with existing pending or approved leave.")
USER 
USER     # 5. Handle substitute logic (BR-EL-014)
USER     if substitute_id is not None:
USER         # Reject if substitute is the applicant
USER         if substitute_id == user_id:
USER             raise LeaveError("Substitute cannot be the applicant.")
USER 
USER         # Check if substitute has overlapping leave of ANY category
USER         sub_apps = LeaveApplication.objects.filter(
USER             user_id=substitute_id,
USER             status__in=[Status.PENDING.value, Status.APPROVED.value],
USER         )
USER 
USER         for sub_app in sub_apps:
USER             try:
USER                 is_overlapping = policy.overlaps(sub_app.start_date, sub_app.end_date, sub_app.half_day, start_date, end_date, half_day)
USER                 if is_overlapping:
USER                     raise LeaveError(f"Substitute {substitute_id} has an overlapping leave application.")
USER             except Exception:
USER                 continue
USER 
USER         # Set stage to SUBSTITUTE
USER         app, created = LeaveApplication.objects.create(
USER             user_id=user_id,
USER             kind=RequestKind.LEAVE.value,
USER             category=category,
USER             start_date=start_date,
USER             end_date=end_date,
USER             half_day=half_day,
USER             purpose=purpose,
USER             station_leave=station_leave,
USER             station_address=station_address,
USER             substitute_id=substitute_id,
USER             unit_head_id=ReportingLine.objects.get(user_id=user_id).unit_head_id,
USER             establishment_id=ReportingLine.objects.get(user_id=user_id).establishment_id,
USER             sanctioning_authority_id=ReportingLine.objects.get(user_id=user_id).sanctioning_authority_id,
USER         )
USER 
USER         app.stage = Stage.SUBSTITUTE.value
USER         app.assignee_id = substitute_id
USER         app.substitute_status = SubstituteStatus.PENDING.value
USER     else:
USER         # No substitute nominated
USER         app, created = LeaveApplication.objects.create(
USER             user_id=user_id,
USER             kind=RequestKind.LEAVE.value,
USER             category=category,
USER             start_date=start_date,
USER             end_date=end_date,
USER             half_day=half_day,
USER             purpose=purpose,
USER             station_leave=station_leave,
USER             station_address=station_address,
USER             substitute_id=None,
USER             unit_head_id=ReportingLine.objects.get(user_id=user_id).unit_head_id,
USER             establishment_id=ReportingLine.objects.get(user_id=user_id).establishment_id,
USER             sanctioning_authority_id=ReportingLine.objects.get(user_id=user_id).sanctioning_authority_id,
USER         )
USER 
USER     # 6. Load ReportingLine (BR-EL-018) - Already done above via create defaults
USER     
USER     # 7. Route to approver (BR-EL-015, BR-EL-020)
USER     route_to_approver(app)
USER 
USER     # Log action
USER     log_action(app, user_id, "apply", f"Leave application submitted for {category} leave.")
USER 
USER     return app
USER 
USER 
USER def respond_to_substitute(actor_id: int, application_id: int, accept: bool) -> LeaveApplication:
USER     """Accept or decline a substitute request.
USER 
USER     Only the current assignee (substitute) may respond.
USER     """
USER     app = LeaveApplication.objects.select_for_update().get(id=application_id)
USER 
USER     if app.assignee_id != actor_id:
USER         raise NotFound(f"Only the assigned substitute can respond to this application.")
USER 
USER     if app.status not in [Status.PENDING.value, Stage.SUBSTITUTE.value]:  # Assuming 'pending' or 'submitted' states allow response
USER         raise NotFound(f"Cannot respond to substitute request for application in status {app.status}.")
USER 
USER     old_status = app.status
USER     if accept:
USER         new_status = Status.PENDING.value  # Keep pending but mark substitute accepted
USER         description = "Substitute consent accepted."
USER         app.substitute_status = SubstituteStatus.ACCEPTED.value
USER         # Route to approver after acceptance
USER         route_to_approver(app)
USER     else:
USER         new_status = Status.PENDING.value  # Revert to applicant action required state
USER         description = "Substitute consent declined."
USER         app.substitute_status = SubstituteStatus.DECLINED.value
USER         app.assignee_id = None
USER 
USER     app.save()
USER 
USER     log_action(app, actor_id, "substitute_response", description)
USER 
USER     return app
USER 
USER 
USER def replace_substitute(actor_id: int, application_id: int, substitute_id: int) -> LeaveApplication:
USER     """Nominate a replacement substitute after a decline.
USER 
USER     Only the owner (applicant) may replace a declined substitute.
USER     """
USER     app = LeaveApplication.objects.select_for_update().get(id=application_id)
USER 
USER     if app.user_id != actor_id:
USER         raise NotFound(f"Only the application owner can nominate a replacement substitute.")
USER 
USER     if app.status not in [Status.PENDING.value]:
USER         raise NotFound(f"Can only replace a declined substitute. Current status: {app.status}.")
USER 
USER     if app.substitute_status != SubstituteStatus.DECLINED.value:
USER         raise NotFound(f"Can only replace a declined substitute. Current status: {app.substitute_status}.")
USER 
USER     # Validate new substitute eligibility (simplified: not applicant, no overlap)
USER     if substitute_id == actor_id:
USER         raise LeaveError("Substitute cannot be the applicant.")
USER 
USER     # Check for existing pending/approved leave of the new substitute that overlaps
USER     sub_apps = LeaveApplication.objects.filter(
USER         user_id=substitute_id,
USER         status__in=[Status.PENDING.value, Status.APPROVED.value],
USER     )
USER 
USER     for sub_app in sub_apps:
USER         try:
USER             is_overlapping = policy.overlaps(sub_app.start_date, sub_app.end_date, sub_app.half_day, app.start_date, app.end_date, app.half_day)
USER             if is_overlapping:
USER                 raise LeaveError(f"New substitute {substitute_id} has an overlapping leave application.")
USER         except Exception:
USER             continue
USER 
USER     # Update the nominated substitute ID and status
USER     app.substitute_id = substitute_id
USER     app.substitute_status = SubstituteStatus.PENDING.value
USER     app.stage = Stage.SUBSTITUTE.value
USER     app.assignee_id = substitute_id
USER     app.save()
USER 
USER     log_action(app, actor_id, "replace_substitute", f"New substitute {substitute_id} nominated.")
USER 
USER     return app
USER 
USER 
USER def withdraw(actor_id: int, application_id: int) -> LeaveApplication:
USER     """Withdraw a pending leave request.
USER 
USER     Only the owner may withdraw a PENDING request.
USER     """
USER     app = LeaveApplication.objects.select_for_update().get(id=application_id)
USER 
USER     if app.user_id != actor_id:
USER         raise NotFound(f"Only the application owner can withdraw this application.")
USER 
USER     if app.status not in [Status.PENDING.value]:
USER         raise NotFound(f"Cannot withdraw application in status {app.status}.")
USER 
USER     old_status = app.status
USER     app.status = Status.WITHDRAWN.value
USER     app.assignee_id = None
USER     app.save()
USER 
USER     log_action(app, actor_id, "withdraw", f"Application withdrawn by user {actor_id}.")
USER 
USER     return app
USER ```

USER class LeaveError(BadRequestError):
USER     code = "leave_error"

USER class NotFound(NotFoundError):

USER def log_action(app: LeaveApplication, actor_id: int, action: str, remark: str = "") -> None:
USER     """Append an entry to the leave action history."""
USER     LeaveAction.objects.create(
USER         application=app,
USER         actor_id=actor_id,

USER         remark=remark,
USER     )

USER def get_balance(user_id: int, kind: str, category: str, year: int) -> LeaveBalance:
USER     """Get or create leave balance for a user.
USER 
USER     Raises LeaveError if the user is not eligible for the category/kind combination.
USER     """
USER     try:
USER         entitlement = policy.entitlement(category, kind)
USER     except policy.PolicyError as e:
USER         raise LeaveError(str(e))
USER 
USER     if entitlement is None:
USER         raise LeaveError(f"Not eligible for {category} leave as {kind}.")
USER 
USER     balance, created = LeaveBalance.objects.get_or_create(
USER         user_id=user_id,
USER         year=year,
USER         category=category,
USER         defaults={
USER             "entitled": policy.ENTITLEMENT[category][0],
USER             "used": Decimal("0"),
USER         },
USER     )
USER 
USER     if not created:
USER         # Ensure entitled matches policy (in case of manual edits or data sync issues)
USER         balance.entitled = policy.ENTITLEMENT[category][0]
USER         balance.save()
USER 
USER     return balance
USER 
USER 
USER def has_overlap(user_id: int, start: date, end: date, half_day: str, exclude_id: Optional[int] = None) -> bool:
USER     """Check if the user has an overlapping leave application.
USER 
USER     Considers PENDING or APPROVED LEAVE-kind applications.
USER     Applies BR-EL-012 exception for CL half-days on single dates.
USER     """
USER     from datetime import date as DateType
USER     
USER     # Determine which categories count as "LEAVE" for overlap purposes (excluding CANCELLATION)
USER     leave_categories = [cat[0] for cat in LeaveApplication._meta.get_field('category').choices if cat[0] != 'CANCELLATION']
USER 
USER     applications = LeaveApplication.objects.filter(
USER         user_id=user_id,
USER         category__in=leave_categories,
USER         status__in=[Status.PENDING.value, Status.APPROVED.value],
USER     )
USER 
USER     # Exclude the current application if provided
USER     if exclude_id is not None:
USER         applications = applications.exclude(id=exclude_id)
USER 
USER     for app in applications:
USER         # Calculate overlap using policy logic
USER         try:
USER             is_overlapping = policy.overlaps(app.start_date, app.end_date, app.half_day, start, end, half_day)
USER             if is_overlapping:
USER                 return True
USER         except Exception:
USER             continue
USER 
USER     return False
USER 
USER 
USER def route_to_approver(app: LeaveApplication) -> None:
USER     """Route the application to the appropriate approver based on hierarchy.
USER 
USER     - If unit_head_id == user_id (self-sanctioning authority): go to SANCTION.
USER     - If no sanctioning_authority_id: self-sanction (SANCTION).
USER     - Otherwise: route to UNIT_HEAD first, then ESTABLISHMENT, then SANCTION.
USER     """
USER     # Determine the initial assignee based on category and hierarchy
USER     if app.unit_head_id == app.user_id:
USER         # Self-sanctioning authority (e.g., Director)
USER         app.stage = Stage.SANCTION.value
USER         app.assignee_id = app.sanctioning_authority_id or app.user_id
USER     else:
USER         # Standard hierarchy: Unit Head -> Establishment -> Sanctioning Authority
USER         # Per DESIGN.md, we route to UNIT_HEAD first for review/recommendation
USER         app.stage = Stage.UNIT_HEAD.value
USER         app.assignee_id = app.unit_head_id
USER 
USER     app.save()
USER 
USER 
USER def apply_leave(user_id: int, kind: str, data: dict) -> LeaveApplication:
USER     """Create and submit a new leave application.
USER 
USER     Validates eligibility, balance, overlaps, and substitutes.
USER     Routes the application to the appropriate approver.
USER     """
USER     category = data["category"]
USER     start_date = data["start_date"]
USER     end_date = data["end_date"]
USER     half_day = data.get("half_day", "none")
USER     purpose = data["purpose"]
USER     station_leave = data.get("station_leave", False)
USER     station_address = data.get("station_address", "")
USER     substitute_id = data.get("substitute_id")
USER 
USER     # 1. Check eligibility (BR-EL-001, BR-EL-005, etc.)
USER     try:
USER         entitlement = policy.entitlement(category, kind)
USER     except policy.PolicyError as e:
USER         raise LeaveError(str(e))
USER 
USER     if entitlement is None:
USER         raise LeaveError(f"Not eligible for {category} leave as {kind}.")
USER 
USER     # 2. Compute days (BR-EL-010, BR-EL-011)
USER     try:
USER         days = policy.count_days(category, start_date, end_date, half_day)
USER     except policy.PolicyError as e:
USER         raise LeaveError(str(e))
USER 
USER     if days <= 0:
USER         raise LeaveError(f"Invalid leave duration: {days} days.")
USER 
USER     # 3. Check balance (BR-EL-025)
USER     try:
USER         balance = get_balance(user_id, kind, category, start_date.year)
USER     except LeaveError as e:
USER         raise LeaveError(str(e))
USER 
USER     if balance.available < days:
USER         raise LeaveError(f"Insufficient leave balance. Available: {balance.available}, Required: {days}.")
USER 
USER     # 4. Check overlap (BR-EL-012)
USER     if has_overlap(user_id, start_date, end_date, half_day):
USER         raise LeaveError("Leave application overlaps with existing pending or approved leave.")
USER 
USER     # 5. Handle substitute logic (BR-EL-014)
USER     if substitute_id is not None:
USER         # Reject if substitute is the applicant
USER         if substitute_id == user_id:
USER             raise LeaveError("Substitute cannot be the applicant.")
USER 
USER         # Check if substitute has overlapping leave of ANY category
USER         sub_apps = LeaveApplication.objects.filter(
USER             user_id=substitute_id,
USER             status__in=[Status.PENDING.value, Status.APPROVED.value],
USER         )
USER 
USER         for sub_app in sub_apps:
USER             try:
USER                 is_overlapping = policy.overlaps(sub_app.start_date, sub_app.end_date, sub_app.half_day, start_date, end_date, half_day)
USER                 if is_overlapping:
USER                     raise LeaveError(f"Substitute {substitute_id} has an overlapping leave application.")
USER             except Exception:
USER                 continue
USER 
USER         # Set stage to SUBSTITUTE
USER         line = ReportingLine.objects.filter(user_id=user_id).first()
USER         if line is None:
USER             raise LeaveError("No reporting line configured.")
USER 
USER         app = LeaveApplication.objects.create(
USER             user_id=user_id,
USER             kind=RequestKind.LEAVE.value,
USER             category=category,
USER             start_date=start_date,
USER             end_date=end_date,
USER             half_day=half_day,
USER             days=days,
USER             purpose=purpose,
USER             station_leave=station_leave,
USER             station_address=station_address,
USER             substitute_id=substitute_id,
USER             unit_head_id=line.unit_head_id,
USER             establishment_id=line.establishment_id,
USER             sanctioning_authority_id=line.sanctioning_authority_id,
USER             status=Status.PENDING,
USER         )
USER 
USER         app.stage = Stage.SUBSTITUTE.value
USER         app.assignee_id = substitute_id
USER         app.substitute_status = SubstituteStatus.PENDING.value
USER         app.save()
USER     else:
USER         # No substitute nominated
USER         line = ReportingLine.objects.filter(user_id=user_id).first()
USER         if line is None:
USER             raise LeaveError("No reporting line configured.")
USER 
USER         app = LeaveApplication.objects.create(
USER             user_id=user_id,
USER             kind=RequestKind.LEAVE.value,
USER             category=category,
USER             start_date=start_date,
USER             end_date=end_date,
USER             half_day=half_day,
USER             days=days,
USER             purpose=purpose,
USER             station_leave=station_leave,
USER             station_address=station_address,
USER             substitute_id=None,
USER             unit_head_id=line.unit_head_id,
USER             establishment_id=line.establishment_id,
USER             sanctioning_authority_id=line.sanctioning_authority_id,
USER             status=Status.PENDING,
USER         )
USER 
USER         app.substitute_status = SubstituteStatus.NOT_REQUIRED.value
USER         route_to_approver(app)
USER 
USER     # Log action
USER     log_action(app, user_id, "apply", f"Leave application submitted for {category} leave.")
USER 
USER     return app
USER 
USER 
USER def respond_to_substitute(actor_id: int, application_id: int, accept: bool) -> LeaveApplication:
USER     """Accept or decline a substitute request.
USER 
USER     Only the current assignee (substitute) may respond.
USER     """
USER     app = LeaveApplication.objects.select_for_update().get(id=application_id)
USER 
USER     if app.assignee_id != actor_id:
USER         raise NotFound(f"Only the assigned substitute can respond to this application.")
USER 
USER     if app.status not in [Status.PENDING.value, Stage.SUBSTITUTE.value]:  # Assuming 'pending' or 'submitted' states allow response
USER         raise NotFound(f"Cannot respond to substitute request for application in status {app.status}.")
USER 
USER     old_status = app.status
USER     if accept:
USER         new_status = Status.PENDING.value  # Keep pending but mark substitute accepted
USER         description = "Substitute consent accepted."
USER         app.substitute_status = SubstituteStatus.ACCEPTED.value
USER         # Route to approver after acceptance
USER         route_to_approver(app)
USER     else:
USER         new_status = Status.PENDING.value  # Revert to applicant action required state
USER         description = "Substitute consent declined."
USER         app.substitute_status = SubstituteStatus.DECLINED.value
USER         app.assignee_id = None
USER 
USER     app.save()
USER 
USER     log_action(app, actor_id, "substitute_response", description)
USER 
USER     return app
USER 
USER 
USER def replace_substitute(actor_id: int, application_id: int, substitute_id: int) -> LeaveApplication:
USER     """Nominate a replacement substitute after a decline.
USER 
USER     Only the owner (applicant) may replace a declined substitute.
USER     """
USER     app = LeaveApplication.objects.select_for_update().get(id=application_id)
USER 
USER     if app.user_id != actor_id:
USER         raise NotFound(f"Only the application owner can nominate a replacement substitute.")
USER 
USER     if app.status not in [Status.PENDING.value]:
USER         raise NotFound(f"Can only replace a declined substitute. Current status: {app.status}.")
USER 
USER     if app.substitute_status != SubstituteStatus.DECLINED.value:
USER         raise NotFound(f"Can only replace a declined substitute. Current status: {app.substitute_status}.")
USER 
USER     # Validate new substitute eligibility (simplified: not applicant, no overlap)
USER     if substitute_id == actor_id:
USER         raise LeaveError("Substitute cannot be the applicant.")
USER 
USER     # Check for existing pending/approved leave of the new substitute that overlaps
USER     sub_apps = LeaveApplication.objects.filter(
USER         user_id=substitute_id,
USER         status__in=[Status.PENDING.value, Status.APPROVED.value],
USER     )
USER 
USER     for sub_app in sub_apps:
USER         try:
USER             is_overlapping = policy.overlaps(sub_app.start_date, sub_app.end_date, sub_app.half_day, app.start_date, app.end_date, app.half_day)
USER             if is_overlapping:
USER                 raise LeaveError(f"New substitute {substitute_id} has an overlapping leave application.")
USER         except Exception:
USER             continue
USER 
USER     # Update the nominated substitute ID and status
USER     app.substitute_id = substitute_id
USER     app.substitute_status = SubstituteStatus.PENDING.value
USER     app.stage = Stage.SUBSTITUTE.value
USER     app.assignee_id = substitute_id
USER     app.save()
USER 
USER     log_action(app, actor_id, "replace_substitute", f"New substitute {substitute_id} nominated.")
USER 
USER     return app
USER 
USER 
USER def withdraw(actor_id: int, application_id: int) -> LeaveApplication:
USER     """Withdraw a pending leave request.
USER 
USER     Only the owner may withdraw a PENDING request.
USER     """
USER     app = LeaveApplication.objects.select_for_update().get(id=application_id)
USER 
USER     if app.user_id != actor_id:
USER         raise NotFound(f"Only the application owner can withdraw this application.")
USER 
USER     if app.status not in [Status.PENDING.value]:
USER         raise NotFound(f"Cannot withdraw application in status {app.status}.")
USER 
USER     old_status = app.status
USER     app.status = Status.WITHDRAWN.value
USER     app.assignee_id = None
USER     app.save()
USER 
USER     log_action(app, actor_id, "withdraw", f"Application withdrawn by user {actor_id}.")
USER 
USER     return app

USER Review of modules/leave/services/applications.py after task 003b found these defects. Fix only these, with SEARCH/REPLACE edits:
USER 
USER 1. has_overlap builds leave_categories from the field choices and wraps policy.overlaps in `try: ... except Exception: continue`. Replace its body so that it filters LeaveApplication.objects.filter(user_id=user_id, kind=RequestKind.LEAVE, status__in=[Status.PENDING, Status.APPROVED]), applies exclude(id=exclude_id) when exclude_id is not None, and returns True as soon as policy.overlaps(app.start_date, app.end_date, app.half_day, start, end, half_day) is True; otherwise False. No try/except.
USER 
USER 2. In replace_substitute, the loop over sub_apps wraps the overlap check in `try: ... except Exception: continue`, which swallows the LeaveError it raises, so a busy substitute is accepted. Replace the sub_apps query and the whole loop with:
USER     if has_overlap(substitute_id, app.start_date, app.end_date, app.half_day):
USER         raise LeaveError("The substitute is on leave on these dates.")
USER 
USER 3. respond_to_substitute, replace_substitute and withdraw load the row with `LeaveApplication.objects.select_for_update().get(id=application_id)`, which raises DoesNotExist (HTTP 500) for an unknown id. In each of the three, use instead:
USER     app = LeaveApplication.objects.select_for_update().filter(id=application_id).first()
USER     if app is None:
USER         raise NotFound()
USER 
USER 4. respond_to_substitute checks `app.status not in [Status.PENDING.value, Stage.SUBSTITUTE.value]`, which mixes a status with a stage, so a substitute can "respond" to a request that is already with the Unit Head. The condition must be: if app.status != Status.PENDING or app.stage != Stage.SUBSTITUTE: raise NotFound().
USER 
USER 5. Remove the unused lines `from datetime import date as DateType` (they appear in has_overlap and apply_leave) and the unused variables old_status and new_status (in respond_to_substitute and withdraw).

USER 
USER 1. has_overlap builds leave_categories from the field choices and wraps policy.overlaps in `try: ... except Exception: continue`. Replace its body so that it filters LeaveApplication.objects.filter(user_id=user_id, kind=RequestKind.LEAVE, status__in=[Status.PENDING, Status.APPROVED]), applies exclude(id=exclude_id) when exclude_id is not None, and returns True as soon as policy.overlaps(app.start_date, app.end_date, app.half_day, start, end, half_day) is True; otherwise False. No try/except.
USER 
USER 2. In replace_substitute, the loop over sub_apps wraps the overlap check in `try: ... except Exception: continue`, which swallows the LeaveError it raises, so a busy substitute is accepted. Replace the sub_apps query and the whole loop with:
USER     if has_overlap(substitute_id, app.start_date, app.end_date, app.half_day):
USER         raise LeaveError("The substitute is on leave on these dates.")
USER 
USER 3. respond_to_substitute, replace_substitute and withdraw load the row with `LeaveApplication.objects.select_for_update().get(id=application_id)`, which raises DoesNotExist (HTTP 500) for an unknown id. In each of the three, use instead:
USER     app = LeaveApplication.objects.select_for_update().filter(id=application_id).first()
USER     if app is None:
USER         raise NotFound()
USER 
USER 4. respond_to_substitute checks `app.status not in [Status.PENDING.value, Stage.SUBSTITUTE.value]`, which mixes a status with a stage, so a substitute can "respond" to a request that is already with the Unit Head. The condition must be: if app.status != Status.PENDING or app.stage != Stage.SUBSTITUTE: raise NotFound().

ASSISTANT     """Check if the user has an overlapping leave application.
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     Considers PENDING or APPROVED LEAVE-kind applications.
ASSISTANT     Applies BR-EL-012 exception for CL half-days on single dates.
ASSISTANT     """
ASSISTANT     from datetime import date as DateType
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     # Determine which categories count as "LEAVE" for overlap purposes (excluding CANCELLATION)
ASSISTANT     leave_categories = [cat[0] for cat in LeaveApplication._meta.get_field('category').choices if cat[0] != 'CANCELLATION']

ASSISTANT         category__in=leave_categories,
ASSISTANT         status__in=[Status.PENDING.value, Status.APPROVED.value],

ASSISTANT def respond_to_substitute(actor_id: int, application_id: int, accept: bool) -> LeaveApplication:
ASSISTANT     """Accept or decline a substitute request.
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     Only the current assignee (substitute) may respond.
ASSISTANT     """

ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     if app.assignee_id != actor_id:
ASSISTANT         raise NotFound(f"Only the assigned substitute can respond to this application.")
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     if app.status not in [Status.PENDING.value, Stage.SUBSTITUTE.value]:  # Assuming 'pending' or 'submitted' states allow response
ASSISTANT         raise NotFound(f"Cannot respond to substitute request for application in status {app.status}.")
ASSISTANT

ASSISTANT     if accept:
ASSISTANT         new_status = Status.PENDING.value  # Keep pending but mark substitute accepted
ASSISTANT         description = "Substitute consent accepted."

ASSISTANT         # Route to approver after acceptance
ASSISTANT         route_to_approver(app)
ASSISTANT     else:

ASSISTANT         app.assignee_id = None
ASSISTANT

ASSISTANT def replace_substitute(actor_id: int, application_id: int, substitute_id: int) -> LeaveApplication:
ASSISTANT     """Nominate a replacement substitute after a decline.
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     Only the owner (applicant) may replace a declined substitute.
ASSISTANT     """
ASSISTANT     app = LeaveApplication.objects.select_for_update().get(id=application_id)
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     if app.user_id != actor_id:
ASSISTANT         raise NotFound(f"Only the application owner can nominate a replacement substitute.")
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     if app.status not in [Status.PENDING.value]:
ASSISTANT         raise NotFound(f"Can only replace a declined substitute. Current status: {app.status}.")
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     if app.substitute_status != SubstituteStatus.DECLINED.value:
ASSISTANT         raise NotFound(f"Can only replace a declined substitute. Current status: {app.substitute_status}.")
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     # Validate new substitute eligibility (simplified: not applicant, no overlap)
ASSISTANT     if substitute_id == actor_id:
ASSISTANT         raise LeaveError("Substitute cannot be the applicant.")
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     # Check for existing pending/approved leave of the new substitute that overlaps
ASSISTANT     sub_apps = LeaveApplication.objects.filter(
ASSISTANT         user_id=substitute_id,
ASSISTANT         status__in=[Status.PENDING.value, Status.APPROVED.value],
ASSISTANT     )
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     for sub_app in sub_apps:
ASSISTANT         try:
ASSISTANT             is_overlapping = policy.overlaps(sub_app.start_date, sub_app.end_date, sub_app.half_day, app.start_date, app.end_date, app.half_day)
ASSISTANT             if is_overlapping:
ASSISTANT                 raise LeaveError(f"New substitute {substitute_id} has an overlapping leave application.")
ASSISTANT         except Exception:
ASSISTANT             continue
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     # Update the nominated substitute ID and status
ASSISTANT     app.substitute_id = substitute_id
ASSISTANT     app.substitute_status = SubstituteStatus.PENDING.value
ASSISTANT     app.stage = Stage.SUBSTITUTE.value
ASSISTANT     app.assignee_id = substitute_id
ASSISTANT     app.save()
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     log_action(app, actor_id, "replace_substitute", f"New substitute {substitute_id} nominated.")
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     return app

USER ```
USER from datetime import date
USER from decimal import Decimal
USER from typing import Optional
USER 
USER from django.db import transaction
USER 
USER from core.api.exceptions import BadRequestError, NotFoundError
USER from modules.leave.domain import policy
USER from modules.leave.models import (
USER     LeaveAction, LeaveApplication, LeaveBalance, ReportingLine,
USER     RequestKind, Stage, Status, SubstituteStatus,
USER )
USER 
USER 
USER class LeaveError(BadRequestError):
USER     code = "leave_error"
USER 
USER 
USER class NotFound(NotFoundError):
USER     pass
USER 
USER 
USER def log_action(app: LeaveApplication, actor_id: int, action: str, remark: str = "") -> None:
USER     """Append an entry to the leave action history."""
USER     LeaveAction.objects.create(
USER         application=app,
USER         actor_id=actor_id,
USER         action=action,
USER         remark=remark,
USER     )
USER 
USER 
USER def get_balance(user_id: int, kind: str, category: str, year: int) -> LeaveBalance:
USER     """Get or create leave balance for a user.
USER 
USER     Raises LeaveError if the user is not eligible for the category/kind combination.
USER     """
USER     try:
USER         entitlement = policy.entitlement(category, kind)
USER     except policy.PolicyError as e:
USER         raise LeaveError(str(e))
USER 
USER     if entitlement is None:
USER         raise LeaveError(f"Not eligible for {category} leave as {kind}.")
USER 
USER     balance, created = LeaveBalance.objects.get_or_create(
USER         user_id=user_id,
USER         year=year,
USER         category=category,
USER         defaults={
USER             "entitled": policy.ENTITLEMENT[category][0],
USER             "used": Decimal("0"),
USER         },
USER     )
USER 
USER     if not created:
USER         # Ensure entitled matches policy (in case of manual edits or data sync issues)
USER         balance.entitled = policy.ENTITLEMENT[category][0]
USER         balance.save()
USER 
USER     return balance
USER 
USER 
USER def has_overlap(user_id: int, start: date, end: date, half_day: str, exclude_id: Optional[int] = None) -> bool:
USER     """Check if the user has an overlapping leave application.
USER 
USER     Considers PENDING or APPROVED LEAVE-kind applications.
USER     Applies BR-EL-012 exception for CL half-days on single dates.
USER     """
USER     # Determine which categories count as "LEAVE" for overlap purposes (excluding CANCELLATION)
USER     leave_categories = [cat[0] for cat in LeaveApplication._meta.get_field('category').choices if cat[0] != 'CANCELLATION']
USER 
USER     applications = LeaveApplication.objects.filter(
USER         user_id=user_id,
USER         kind=RequestKind.LEAVE.value,
USER         category__in=leave_categories,
USER         status__in=[Status.PENDING.value, Status.APPROVED.value],
USER     )
USER 
USER     # Exclude the current application if provided
USER     if exclude_id is not None:
USER         applications = applications.exclude(id=exclude_id)
USER 
USER     for app in applications:
USER         # Calculate overlap using policy logic
USER         is_overlapping = policy.overlaps(app.start_date, app.end_date, app.half_day, start, end, half_day)
USER         if is_overlapping:
USER             return True
USER 
USER     return False
USER 
USER 
USER def route_to_approver(app: LeaveApplication) -> None:
USER     """Route the application to the appropriate approver based on hierarchy.
USER 
USER     - If unit_head_id == user_id (self-sanctioning authority): go to SANCTION.
USER     - If no sanctioning_authority_id: self-sanction (SANCTION).
USER     - Otherwise: route to UNIT_HEAD first, then ESTABLISHMENT, then SANCTION.
USER     """
USER     # Determine the initial assignee based on category and hierarchy
USER     if app.unit_head_id == app.user_id:
USER         # Self-sanctioning authority (e.g., Director)
USER         app.stage = Stage.SANCTION.value
USER         app.assignee_id = app.sanctioning_authority_id or app.user_id
USER     else:
USER         # Standard hierarchy: Unit Head -> Establishment -> Sanctioning Authority
USER         # Per DESIGN.md, we route to UNIT_HEAD first for review/recommendation
USER         app.stage = Stage.UNIT_HEAD.value
USER         app.assignee_id = app.unit_head_id
USER 
USER     app.save()
USER 
USER 
USER def apply_leave(user_id: int, kind: str, data: dict) -> LeaveApplication:
USER     """Create and submit a new leave application.
USER 
USER     Validates eligibility, balance, overlaps, and substitutes.
USER     Routes the application to the appropriate approver.
USER     """
USER     category = data["category"]
USER     start_date = data["start_date"]
USER     end_date = data["end_date"]
USER     half_day = data.get("half_day", "none")
USER     purpose = data["purpose"]
USER     station_leave = data.get("station_leave", False)
USER     station_address = data.get("station_address", "")
USER     substitute_id = data.get("substitute_id")
USER 
USER     # 1. Check eligibility (BR-EL-001, BR-EL-005, etc.)
USER     try:
USER         entitlement = policy.entitlement(category, kind)
USER     except policy.PolicyError as e:
USER         raise LeaveError(str(e))
USER 
USER     if entitlement is None:
USER         raise LeaveError(f"Not eligible for {category} leave as {kind}.")
USER 
USER     # 2. Compute days (BR-EL-010, BR-EL-011)
USER     try:
USER         days = policy.count_days(category, start_date, end_date, half_day)
USER     except policy.PolicyError as e:
USER         raise LeaveError(str(e))
USER 
USER     if days <= 0:
USER         raise LeaveError(f"Invalid leave duration: {days} days.")
USER 
USER     # 3. Check balance (BR-EL-025)
USER     try:
USER         balance = get_balance(user_id, kind, category, start_date.year)
USER     except LeaveError as e:
USER         raise LeaveError(str(e))
USER 
USER     if balance.available < days:
USER         raise LeaveError(f"Insufficient leave balance. Available: {balance.available}, Required: {days}.")
USER 
USER     # 4. Check overlap (BR-EL-012)
USER     if has_overlap(user_id, start_date, end_date, half_day):
USER         raise LeaveError("Leave application overlaps with existing pending or approved leave.")
USER 
USER     # 5. Handle substitute logic (BR-EL-014)
USER     if substitute_id is not None:
USER         # Reject if substitute is the applicant
USER         if substitute_id == user_id:
USER             raise LeaveError("Substitute cannot be the applicant.")
USER 
USER         # Check if substitute has overlapping leave of ANY category
USER         sub_apps = LeaveApplication.objects.filter(
USER             user_id=substitute_id,
USER             status__in=[Status.PENDING.value, Status.APPROVED.value],
USER         )
USER 
USER         for sub_app in sub_apps:
USER             try:
USER                 is_overlapping = policy.overlaps(sub_app.start_date, sub_app.end_date, sub_app.half_day, start_date, end_date, half_day)
USER                 if is_overlapping:
USER                     raise LeaveError(f"Substitute {substitute_id} has an overlapping leave application.")
USER             except Exception:
USER                 continue
USER 
USER         # Set stage to SUBSTITUTE
USER         line = ReportingLine.objects.filter(user_id=user_id).first()
USER         if line is None:
USER             raise LeaveError("No reporting line configured.")
USER 
USER         app = LeaveApplication.objects.create(
USER             user_id=user_id,
USER             kind=RequestKind.LEAVE.value,
USER             category=category,
USER             start_date=start_date,
USER             end_date=end_date,
USER             half_day=half_day,
USER             days=days,
USER             purpose=purpose,
USER             station_leave=station_leave,
USER             station_address=station_address,
USER             substitute_id=substitute_id,
USER             unit_head_id=line.unit_head_id,
USER             establishment_id=line.establishment_id,
USER             sanctioning_authority_id=line.sanctioning_authority_id,
USER             status=Status.PENDING,
USER         )
USER 
USER         app.stage = Stage.SUBSTITUTE.value
USER         app.assignee_id = substitute_id
USER         app.substitute_status = SubstituteStatus.PENDING.value
USER         app.save()
USER     else:
USER         # No substitute nominated
USER         line = ReportingLine.objects.filter(user_id=user_id).first()
USER         if line is None:
USER             raise LeaveError("No reporting line configured.")
USER 
USER         app = LeaveApplication.objects.create(
USER             user_id=user_id,
USER             kind=RequestKind.LEAVE.value,
USER             category=category,
USER             start_date=start_date,
USER             end_date=end_date,
USER             half_day=half_day,
USER             days=days,
USER             purpose=purpose,
USER             station_leave=station_leave,
USER             station_address=station_address,
USER             substitute_id=None,
USER             unit_head_id=line.unit_head_id,
USER             establishment_id=line.establishment_id,
USER             sanctioning_authority_id=line.sanctioning_authority_id,
USER             status=Status.PENDING,
USER         )
USER 
USER         app.substitute_status = SubstituteStatus.NOT_REQUIRED.value
USER         route_to_approver(app)
USER 
USER     # Log action
USER     log_action(app, user_id, "apply", f"Leave application submitted for {category} leave.")
USER 
USER     return app
USER 
USER 
USER def respond_to_substitute(actor_id: int, application_id: int, accept: bool) -> LeaveApplication:
USER     """Accept or decline a substitute request.
USER 
USER     Only the current assignee (substitute) may respond.
USER     """
USER     app = LeaveApplication.objects.select_for_update().filter(id=application_id).first()
USER     if app is None:
USER         raise NotFound()
USER 
USER     if app.assignee_id != actor_id:
USER         raise NotFound(f"Only the assigned substitute can respond to this application.")
USER

USER         raise NotFound(f"Cannot respond to substitute request for application in status {app.status} and stage {app.stage}.")
USER 
USER     if accept:
USER         description = "Substitute consent accepted."
USER         app.substitute_status = SubstituteStatus.ACCEPTED.value
USER         # Route to approver after acceptance
USER         route_to_approver(app)
USER     else:
USER         description = "Substitute consent declined."
USER         app.substitute_status = SubstituteStatus.DECLINED.value
USER         app.assignee_id = None
USER 
USER     app.save()
USER 
USER     log_action(app, actor_id, "substitute_response", description)
USER 
USER     return app
USER

USER def withdraw(actor_id: int, application_id: int) -> LeaveApplication:
USER     """Withdraw a pending leave request.
USER 
USER     Only the owner may withdraw a PENDING request.
USER     """

USER         raise NotFound(f"Only the application owner can withdraw this application.")

USER         raise NotFound(f"Cannot withdraw application in status {app.status}.")

USER class LeaveBalance(UserScopedModel, TimeStampedModel):
USER     """Per-user, per-year, per-category leave balance."""

USER         max_length=3,

USER 
USER     class Meta:

USER         verbose_name_plural = "Leave Balances"
USER         ordering = ["-year", "-category"]
USER         unique_together = ["user_id", "year", "category"]
USER 
USER     @property
USER     def available(self) -> Decimal:
USER         """Calculate the available leave days."""
USER         return self.entitled - self.used
USER 
USER     def __str__(self) -> str:
USER         return f"{self.user_id}'s {self.category} balance for {self.year}"

USER from datetime import date
USER from decimal import Decimal
USER from typing import Optional
USER 
USER from django.db import transaction
USER 
USER from core.api.exceptions import BadRequestError, NotFoundError
USER from modules.leave.domain import policy
USER from modules.leave.models import (
USER     LeaveAction, LeaveApplication, LeaveBalance, ReportingLine,
USER     RequestKind, Stage, Status, SubstituteStatus,
USER )
USER 
USER 
USER class LeaveError(BadRequestError):
USER     code = "leave_error"
USER 
USER 
USER class NotFound(NotFoundError):
USER     pass
USER 
USER 
USER def log_action(app: LeaveApplication, actor_id: int, action: str, remark: str = "") -> None:
USER     """Append an entry to the leave action history."""
USER     LeaveAction.objects.create(
USER         application=app,
USER         actor_id=actor_id,
USER         action=action,
USER         remark=remark,
USER     )
USER 
USER 
USER def get_balance(user_id: int, kind: str, category: str, year: int) -> LeaveBalance:
USER     """Get or create leave balance for a user.
USER 
USER     Raises LeaveError if the user is not eligible for the category/kind combination.
USER     """
USER     try:
USER         entitlement = policy.entitlement(category, kind)
USER     except policy.PolicyError as e:
USER         raise LeaveError(str(e))
USER 
USER     if entitlement is None:
USER         raise LeaveError(f"Not eligible for {category} leave as {kind}.")
USER 
USER     balance, created = LeaveBalance.objects.get_or_create(
USER         user_id=user_id,
USER         year=year,
USER         category=category,
USER         defaults={
USER             "entitled": policy.ENTITLEMENT[category][0],
USER             "used": Decimal("0"),
USER         },
USER     )
USER 
USER     if not created:
USER         # Ensure entitled matches policy (in case of manual edits or data sync issues)
USER         balance.entitled = policy.ENTITLEMENT[category][0]
USER         balance.save()
USER 
USER     return balance
USER 
USER 
USER def has_overlap(user_id: int, start: date, end: date, half_day: str, exclude_id: Optional[int] = None) -> bool:
USER     """Check if the user has an overlapping leave application.
USER 
USER     Considers PENDING or APPROVED LEAVE-kind applications.
USER     Applies BR-EL-012 exception for CL half-days on single dates.
USER     """
USER     # Determine which categories count as "LEAVE" for overlap purposes (excluding CANCELLATION)
USER     leave_categories = [cat[0] for cat in LeaveApplication._meta.get_field('category').choices if cat[0] != 'CANCELLATION']
USER 
USER     applications = LeaveApplication.objects.filter(
USER         user_id=user_id,
USER         kind=RequestKind.LEAVE.value,
USER         category__in=leave_categories,
USER         status__in=[Status.PENDING.value, Status.APPROVED.value],
USER     )
USER 
USER     # Exclude the current application if provided
USER     if exclude_id is not None:
USER         applications = applications.exclude(id=exclude_id)
USER 
USER     for app in applications:
USER         # Calculate overlap using policy logic
USER         is_overlapping = policy.overlaps(app.start_date, app.end_date, app.half_day, start, end, half_day)
USER         if is_overlapping:
USER             return True
USER 
USER     return False
USER 
USER 
USER def route_to_approver(app: LeaveApplication) -> None:
USER     """Route the application to the appropriate approver based on hierarchy.
USER 
USER     - If unit_head_id == user_id (self-sanctioning authority): go to SANCTION.
USER     - If no sanctioning_authority_id: self-sanction (SANCTION).
USER     - Otherwise: route to UNIT_HEAD first, then ESTABLISHMENT, then SANCTION.
USER     """
USER     # Determine the initial assignee based on category and hierarchy
USER     if app.unit_head_id == app.user_id:
USER         # Self-sanctioning authority (e.g., Director)
USER         app.stage = Stage.SANCTION.value
USER         app.assignee_id = app.sanctioning_authority_id or app.user_id
USER     else:
USER         # Standard hierarchy: Unit Head -> Establishment -> Sanctioning Authority
USER         # Per DESIGN.md, we route to UNIT_HEAD first for review/recommendation
USER         app.stage = Stage.UNIT_HEAD.value
USER         app.assignee_id = app.unit_head_id
USER 
USER     app.save()
USER 
USER 
USER def apply_leave(user_id: int, kind: str, data: dict) -> LeaveApplication:
USER     """Create and submit a new leave application.
USER 
USER     Validates eligibility, balance, overlaps, and substitutes.
USER     Routes the application to the appropriate approver.
USER     """
USER     category = data["category"]
USER     start_date = data["start_date"]
USER     end_date = data["end_date"]
USER     half_day = data.get("half_day", "none")
USER     purpose = data["purpose"]
USER     station_leave = data.get("station_leave", False)
USER     station_address = data.get("station_address", "")
USER     substitute_id = data.get("substitute_id")
USER 
USER     # 1. Check eligibility (BR-EL-001, BR-EL-005, etc.)
USER     try:
USER         entitlement = policy.entitlement(category, kind)
USER     except policy.PolicyError as e:
USER         raise LeaveError(str(e))
USER 
USER     if entitlement is None:
USER         raise LeaveError(f"Not eligible for {category} leave as {kind}.")
USER 
USER     # 2. Compute days (BR-EL-010, BR-EL-011)
USER     try:
USER         days = policy.count_days(category, start_date, end_date, half_day)
USER     except policy.PolicyError as e:
USER         raise LeaveError(str(e))
USER 
USER     if days <= 0:
USER         raise LeaveError(f"Invalid leave duration: {days} days.")
USER 
USER     # 3. Check balance (BR-EL-025)
USER     try:
USER         balance = get_balance(user_id, kind, category, start_date.year)
USER     except LeaveError as e:
USER         raise LeaveError(str(e))
USER 
USER     if balance.available < days:
USER         raise LeaveError(f"Insufficient leave balance. Available: {balance.available}, Required: {days}.")
USER 
USER     # 4. Check overlap (BR-EL-012)
USER     if has_overlap(user_id, start_date, end_date, half_day):
USER         raise LeaveError("Leave application overlaps with existing pending or approved leave.")
USER 
USER     # 5. Handle substitute logic (BR-EL-014)
USER     if substitute_id is not None:
USER         # Reject if substitute is the applicant
USER         if substitute_id == user_id:
USER             raise LeaveError("Substitute cannot be the applicant.")
USER 
USER         # Check if substitute has overlapping leave of ANY category
USER         sub_apps = LeaveApplication.objects.filter(
USER             user_id=substitute_id,
USER             status__in=[Status.PENDING.value, Status.APPROVED.value],
USER         )
USER 
USER         for sub_app in sub_apps:
USER             try:
USER                 is_overlapping = policy.overlaps(sub_app.start_date, sub_app.end_date, sub_app.half_day, start_date, end_date, half_day)
USER                 if is_overlapping:
USER                     raise LeaveError(f"Substitute {substitute_id} has an overlapping leave application.")
USER             except Exception:
USER                 continue
USER 
USER         # Set stage to SUBSTITUTE
USER         line = ReportingLine.objects.filter(user_id=user_id).first()
USER         if line is None:
USER             raise LeaveError("No reporting line configured.")
USER 
USER         app = LeaveApplication.objects.create(
USER             user_id=user_id,
USER             kind=RequestKind.LEAVE.value,
USER             category=category,
USER             start_date=start_date,
USER             end_date=end_date,
USER             half_day=half_day,
USER             days=days,
USER             purpose=purpose,
USER             station_leave=station_leave,
USER             station_address=station_address,
USER             substitute_id=substitute_id,
USER             unit_head_id=line.unit_head_id,
USER             establishment_id=line.establishment_id,
USER             sanctioning_authority_id=line.sanctioning_authority_id,
USER             status=Status.PENDING,
USER         )
USER 
USER         app.stage = Stage.SUBSTITUTE.value
USER         app.assignee_id = substitute_id
USER         app.substitute_status = SubstituteStatus.PENDING.value
USER         app.save()
USER     else:
USER         # No substitute nominated
USER         line = ReportingLine.objects.filter(user_id=user_id).first()
USER         if line is None:
USER             raise LeaveError("No reporting line configured.")
USER 
USER         app = LeaveApplication.objects.create(
USER             user_id=user_id,
USER             kind=RequestKind.LEAVE.value,
USER             category=category,
USER             start_date=start_date,
USER             end_date=end_date,
USER             half_day=half_day,
USER             days=days,
USER             purpose=purpose,
USER             station_leave=station_leave,
USER             station_address=station_address,
USER             substitute_id=None,
USER             unit_head_id=line.unit_head_id,
USER             establishment_id=line.establishment_id,
USER             sanctioning_authority_id=line.sanctioning_authority_id,
USER             status=Status.PENDING,
USER         )
USER 
USER         app.substitute_status = SubstituteStatus.NOT_REQUIRED.value
USER         route_to_approver(app)
USER 
USER     # Log action
USER     log_action(app, user_id, "apply", f"Leave application submitted for {category} leave.")
USER 
USER     return app
USER 
USER 
USER def respond_to_substitute(actor_id: int, application_id: int, accept: bool) -> LeaveApplication:
USER     """Accept or decline a substitute request.
USER 
USER     Only the current assignee (substitute) may respond.
USER     """
USER     app = LeaveApplication.objects.select_for_update().filter(id=application_id).first()
USER     if app is None:
USER         raise NotFound()
USER 
USER     if app.assignee_id != actor_id:
USER         raise NotFound(f"Only the assigned substitute can respond to this application.")
USER 
USER     if app.status != Status.PENDING.value or app.stage != Stage.SUBSTITUTE.value:
USER         raise NotFound(f"Cannot respond to substitute request for application in status {app.status} and stage {app.stage}.")
USER 
USER     if accept:
USER         description = "Substitute consent accepted."
USER         app.substitute_status = SubstituteStatus.ACCEPTED.value
USER         # Route to approver after acceptance
USER         route_to_approver(app)
USER     else:
USER         description = "Substitute consent declined."
USER         app.substitute_status = SubstituteStatus.DECLINED.value
USER         app.assignee_id = None
USER 
USER     app.save()
USER 
USER     log_action(app, actor_id, "substitute_response", description)
USER 
USER     return app
USER 
USER 
USER def replace_substitute(actor_id: int, application_id: int, substitute_id: int) -> LeaveApplication:
USER     """Nominate a replacement substitute after a decline.
USER 
USER     Only the owner (applicant) may replace a declined substitute.
USER     """
USER     app = LeaveApplication.objects.select_for_update().get(id=application_id)
USER 
USER     if app.user_id != actor_id:
USER         raise NotFound(f"Only the application owner can nominate a replacement substitute.")
USER 
USER     if app.status not in [Status.PENDING.value]:
USER         raise NotFound(f"Can only replace a declined substitute. Current status: {app.status}.")
USER 
USER     if app.substitute_status != SubstituteStatus.DECLINED.value:
USER         raise NotFound(f"Can only replace a declined substitute. Current status: {app.substitute_status}.")
USER 
USER     # Validate new substitute eligibility (simplified: not applicant, no overlap)
USER     if substitute_id == actor_id:
USER         raise LeaveError("Substitute cannot be the applicant.")
USER 
USER     # Check for existing pending/approved leave of the new substitute that overlaps
USER     if has_overlap(substitute_id, app.start_date, app.end_date, app.half_day):
USER         raise LeaveError("The substitute is on leave on these dates.")
USER 
USER     # Update the nominated substitute ID and status
USER     app.substitute_id = substitute_id
USER     app.substitute_status = SubstituteStatus.PENDING.value
USER     app.stage = Stage.SUBSTITUTE.value
USER     app.assignee_id = substitute_id
USER     app.save()
USER 
USER     log_action(app, actor_id, "replace_substitute", f"New substitute {substitute_id} nominated.")
USER 
USER     return app
USER 
USER 
USER def withdraw(actor_id: int, application_id: int) -> LeaveApplication:
USER     """Withdraw a pending leave request.
USER 
USER     Only the owner may withdraw a PENDING request.
USER     """
USER     app = LeaveApplication.objects.select_for_update().filter(id=application_id).first()
USER     if app is None:
USER         raise NotFound()
USER 
USER     if app.user_id != actor_id:
USER         raise NotFound(f"Only the application owner can withdraw this application.")
USER 
USER     if app.status != Status.PENDING.value:
USER         raise NotFound(f"Cannot withdraw application in status {app.status}.")
USER 
USER     app.status = Status.WITHDRAWN.value
USER     app.assignee_id = None
USER     app.save()
USER 
USER     log_action(app, actor_id, "withdraw", f"Application withdrawn by user {actor_id}.")
USER 
USER     return app
USER ```

USER @transaction.atomic
USER def withdraw_pending_request(
USER     actor_id: int,
USER     application_id: int,
USER ) -> None:
USER     """
USER     Withdraw a pending leave request before final approval.
USER     
USER     Preconditions:
USER     - The application status must be 'draft' or 'submitted' (but not yet approved/rejected/etc).
USER     - Final approval has not occurred.
USER     
USER     Postconditions:
USER     - Application status is set to 'withdrawn'.
USER     - History entry is appended.
USER     
USER     Raises:
USER         ValueError: If the application is already approved, rejected, or in a state where withdrawal is not allowed.
USER     """
USER     application = LeaveApplication.objects.get(id=application_id)
USER 
USER     # Check if withdrawal is allowed (must be pending and not finally approved/rejected)
USER     allowed_statuses = ['draft', 'submitted', 'under_review', 'recommended', 'not_recommended']
USER     
USER     # Exception: If it was recommended/not_recommended by Unit Head, we can still withdraw before final sanction?
USER     # Per EL-UC-007: "Immediately terminate a pending request before final approval."
USER     # "Final approval" implies the decision from the highest competent authority.
USER     # For CL/RH, Unit Head is final. For SCL/EL/COL/VL, Final Sanctioning Authority is final.
USER     # So if status is 'approved' (by Unit Head for CL/RH) or 'recommended' (by Unit Head for SCL/EL...) 
USER     # we need to check who made the decision.
USER     
USER     category_enum = ModelLeaveCategory(application.category)
USER     
USER     # Determine if this is a "final" decision point
USER     # If Unit Head made a final decision (approve/reject), withdrawal is NOT allowed.
USER     # If Unit Head made a recommendation, and it's not yet routed to Final Sanctioning Authority, 
USER     # withdrawal might be allowed? The spec says "before final approval".
USER     # Let's interpret "final approval" as the state where the leave is officially approved/rejected by the highest authority.
USER     # However, EL-UC-007 mentions "prevents later approve/reject actions".
USER     # If status is 'approved' or 'rejected', withdrawal is definitely not allowed.
USER     if application.status in ['approved', 'rejected']:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Cannot withdraw an {application.status} application.")
USER 
USER     # Check for overlap with other active leaves? 
USER     # Usually withdrawal removes the pending leave, so overlap check might be relaxed or handled differently.
USER     # But let's assume we just need to ensure no final decision exists.
USER     
USER     if application.status not in allowed_statuses:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Cannot withdraw application in status {application.status}.")
USER 
USER     # Update status to withdrawn
USER     old_status = application.status
USER     application.status = 'withdrawn'
USER     application.save()
USER 
USER     _log_action_history(
USER         application=application,
USER         actor_id=actor_id,
USER         action_type="withdrawal",
USER         description=f"Application {old_status} withdrawn by employee.",
USER     )

USER @transaction.atomic
USER def request_cancellation(
USER     actor_id: int,
USER     application_id: int,
USER ) -> None:
USER     """
USER     Request cancellation of an approved leave before it starts.
USER     
USER     Preconditions:
USER     - Leave is finally Approved (status 'approved').
USER     - Approved start date has not yet occurred.
USER     
USER     Postconditions:
USER     - A new cancellation application is created with status 'draft'.
USER     - Original application remains unchanged.
USER     - History entry is appended for the original application.
USER     
USER     Raises:
USER         ValueError: If the leave has started or if it's not in 'approved' status.
USER     """
USER     application = LeaveApplication.objects.get(id=application_id)
USER 
USER     category_enum = ModelLeaveCategory(application.category)
USER     
USER     # Check if finally approved
USER     if application.status != 'approved':
USER         raise ValueError(f"Cannot cancel an {application.status} application. Only 'approved' leaves can be cancelled.")
USER     
USER     # Check if start date has passed (simplified: assume current date is known via context or check against a field)
USER     # Since we don't have a 'start_date' field in LeaveApplication, we might need to infer it or rely on business logic elsewhere.
USER     # For now, we assume the service layer handles date checks or we raise if start date is missing/invalid.
USER     # Let's assume there's a way to determine if start date has passed (e.g., via a helper or field).
USER     # If no field exists, we might need to add one, but per instructions, models.py is read-only.
USER     # We'll proceed with the assumption that 'start_date' logic is handled externally or we just check status.
USER     # To be safe, let's assume if start date is not set or passed, we raise. 
USER     # But without a field, we can't check. Let's assume the system knows the current date and we can compare against a stored 'start_date' if it existed.
USER     # Since models.py is read-only, we cannot add fields. We will assume the cancellation request creation implies the start date hasn't passed based on context or a hidden assumption.
USER     # For robustness, let's just check status for now and assume date validation is done by the caller or via a separate service not shown here.
USER     # Actually, EL-UC-008 says "Approved start date has not yet occurred". 
USER     # We'll add a placeholder check that assumes we can determine this (perhaps via a global date context or a missing field assumption).
USER     # To avoid breaking the read-only constraint, we will skip explicit date comparison here and rely on the caller to ensure preconditions.
USER     # However, to be compliant with the spec, let's assume there is a way to know. 
USER     # Let's assume for this implementation that if status is 'approved', we proceed, and the date check is implicit or handled by the system clock vs a stored field (which we can't add).
USER     # We'll raise an error if start date logic fails, but since we can't store it, we'll just proceed.
USER     # Actually, let's assume the 'requested_from' is the start date. If today > requested_from, then it has started.
USER     from datetime import datetime
USER     today = datetime.now().date()
USER     if today > application.requested_from:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Leave has already started on {application.requested_from}. Cancellation is no longer possible.")
USER

USER     # Route it through the same hierarchy as the original leave? 
USER     # EL-UC-008 says "routes the cancellation to the Unit Head and then through the same applicable hierarchy".
USER     # We'll create a draft application with status 'draft' (or maybe 'submitted' immediately?).
USER     # Let's create it as 'draft' so the employee can review before submitting? Or submit directly?
USER     # The spec says "creates the cancellation request". Usually, this means creating an entry.
USER     # We'll create a new LeaveApplication with status 'draft'.

USER         action_type="cancellation_requested",

USER @transaction.atomic
USER def decide_cancellation(
USER     actor_id: int,
USER     application_id: int,
USER     decision: str,
USER ) -> None:
USER     """
USER     Decide on a cancellation request (restore balance if approved).
USER     
USER     Preconditions:
USER     - Cancellation application is in 'submitted' or 'under_review' status.
USER     - Decision is 'approve' or 'reject'.
USER     
USER     Postconditions:

USER     - If rejected: No balance change.

USER     Raises:

USER     """

USER     allowed_statuses = ['submitted', 'under_review']
USER     if cancellation_app.status not in allowed_statuses:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Cannot decide cancellation for application in status {cancellation_app.status}.")
USER 
USER     category_enum = ModelLeaveCategory(cancellation_app.category)
USER     
USER     if decision not in ['approve', 'reject']:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Invalid decision '{decision}'. Must be 'approve' or 'reject'.")
USER

USER     new_status = 'approved' if decision == 'approve' else 'rejected'
USER     cancellation_app.status = new_status

USER         actor_id=actor_id,
USER         action_type="cancellation_decision",
USER         description=f"Cancellation {decision}ed.",
USER     )

USER     # Handle balance restoration on approval (BR-EL-023)
USER     if decision == 'approve':
USER         _restore_balance_on_cancellation(cancellation_app, actor_id)

USER @transaction.atomic
USER def request_extension(
USER     actor_id: int,
USER     application_id: int,
USER     extension_from: date,
USER     extension_to: date,
USER     purpose: str,
USER     station_leave_details: Optional[dict] = None,
USER     nominated_substitute_id: Optional[int] = None,
USER ) -> None:
USER     """
USER     Request an extension of ongoing leave (EL/COL/VL only).
USER     
USER     Preconditions:
USER     - Original leave is ongoing and is EL, COL, or VL.
USER     - Responsibility coverage is required if applicable (handled by substitute consent flow).
USER     
USER     Postconditions:
USER     - A new extension application is created.
USER     - History entry appended.
USER     
USER     Raises:
USER         ValueError: If category is not EL/COL/VL or if validation fails.
USER     """
USER     original_app = LeaveApplication.objects.get(id=application_id)
USER 
USER     category_enum = ModelLeaveCategory(original_app.category)
USER     
USER     # Check if category allows extension (EL, COL, VL)
USER     if category_enum not in [ModelLeaveCategory.EL, ModelLeaveCategory.COL, ModelLeaveCategory.VL]:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Extension is only allowed for EL, COL, or VL categories. Got {category_enum.value}.")
USER 
USER     # Check if original leave is ongoing (status 'approved' and within date range?)
USER     # Simplified: assume status 'approved' means it's active/ongoing until requested_to
USER     if original_app.status != 'approved':
USER         raise ValueError(f"Cannot extend an {original_app.status} application. Only approved leaves can be extended.")
USER 
USER     # Validate dates (simplified)
USER     if extension_from <= original_app.requested_from or extension_to >= original_app.requested_to:
USER         raise ValueError("Extension dates must be within the original leave period.")
USER 
USER     # Check balance? 
USER     # BR-EL-025 mentions updating balance on approval. For extension, we need to ensure sufficient balance for the additional days.
USER     # We'll assume a placeholder check here.
USER     _check_extension_balance(actor_id, category_enum, extension_from, extension_to)
USER 
USER     # Create extension application
USER     # Route it through Unit Head review and higher sanction path?
USER     # EL-UC-009 says "routes it to Unit Head review and the configured higher-sanction path."
USER     # We'll create a new application with status 'draft' (or 'submitted'?).
USER     # Let's create it as 'draft' so it can be submitted.
USER     
USER     extension_app, created = LeaveApplication.objects.get_or_create(
USER         user_id=actor_id,
USER         defaults={
USER             'category': category_enum,
USER             'requested_from': extension_from,
USER             'requested_to': extension_to,
USER             'purpose': purpose,
USER             'station_leave_details': station_leave_details,
USER             'nominated_substitute_id': nominated_substitute_id,
USER             'status': 'draft',
USER         },
USER     )
USER 
USER     if not created:
USER         extension_app.status = 'draft'
USER         extension_app.save()
USER 
USER     # Log history for original application
USER     _log_action_history(
USER         application=original_app,
USER         actor_id=actor_id,
USER         action_type="extension_requested",
USER         description=f"Extension request created from {extension_from} to {extension_to}.",
USER     )
USER 
USER     return extension_app
USER
